- **Stats honesty:** pooled n=12 paired delta −1.29 m (t≈−2.67, significant); per-speed n=6 NOT individually significant (t=−1.42 @9, −2.24 @12). The pooled result + the binary handoff-collapse/recovery are the defensible parts; the per-speed absolute numbers are directional. A larger batch would tighten CIs before any headline.
- **The honesty boundary (disclose):** the fix leans on the GROUND SENSOR emitting a good velocity track — legitimate (the parent project's stereo+EKF / RTK ground rig, ADR-0016/0017 track message, measures velocity) but a real-system REQUIREMENT, not free. And the terminal is still a clean AprilTag. `--cue-latency-comp` is an untested further lever (ARM D, deferred).
- **Decision + strategic call:** ADOPT velocity-emission + dash-unclamp for the FPV/deployment profile. This is very likely the LAST big guidance lever — the analysis puts the theory floor near here, and the remaining gap is now genuinely the FAKED PERCEPTION half. Next honest work is NOT more guidance tuning: it is (a) stress perception AVAILABILITY (the ARM B dash-aborts show degraded cue causes catastrophic failures — quantify the jam/dropout envelope) and/or (b) SHIP THE PORTFOLIO (demo + writeup), disclosing the perception gap as future work.
- **Date:** 2026-07-06. (5-agent analysis workflow + paired Gazebo A/B; worker ran sims, main session Fable synthesized + wrote ADR. --dash-unclamp knob committed 832966c.)

## ADR-0031 — Perception-availability stress envelope: where the intercept breaks under degraded/jammed cue (the honest limitations number)
- **Context:** the portfolio needs an honest "where does it break," not a hand-wave. Gazebo sweep on the ADR-0030 FIX config (running-start + velocity-emission cue + --dash-unclamp, 9 m/s pronav, n=6/arm, master-seed 42, idle machine) varying the cue degradation. All knobs already exist in s2_cue_mock.py (--dropout-p, --link-cutoff-range-m).
- **The envelope:** the intercept HOLDS (≥83% handoff, mean miss ~1.2–1.5 m) as long as EITHER (a) Markov link dropout stays under ~2× nominal (p≤0.24: 6/6 handoff, 1.18 m), OR (b) a jammer cutoff leaves the cue alive to within ~12 m of the target (cutoff@12 m: 6/6, 1.50 m). It BREAKS beyond ~4× dropout (p=0.48: 5/6, 1.95 m) or a cutoff at ~16 m+ (cutoff@16 m: 50%; @20 m: 17%). Cliff between ~12–16 m of true range remaining when the link dies; camera first acquires ~3–10 m, so the cue needs only a few meters' margin past acquisition.
- **THE finding (and a methodology honesty point):** every catastrophic failure is the SAME mode — `DASH: failed to reach handoff range within 20 s`: the mid-course dead-reckon (no --coast-search here) drifts off, the camera never builds its detection streak, and the flight NEVER REACHES ENGAGE. So **perception AVAILABILITY, not terminal guidance accuracy, is the binding constraint once the cue is degraded.** Critically, `miss_m` is logged even for these blind flybys and is deceptively SMALL (a ballistic closest-approach) — averaging raw miss would FLATTER the broken arms (16 m-cutoff's raw mean 1.12 m looks better than baseline despite only 50% ever reaching handoff). **Handoff-rate, not mean miss, is the honest headline metric under degraded perception** — confirms ADR-0030's own warning.
- **New failure mode logged (4× dropout only):** a late, fragile 2-detection handoff that re-loses the tag far out and hard-aborts (`lost tag >5 s far from target`) — post-latch the one-way cue latch (ADR-0010 #5) leaves no fallback. Not previously in the ADR-0018/0030 taxonomy.
- **Honesty caveats:** n=6/arm is thin (brackets the 12–16 m cliff, can't pin it); the `--link-cutoff-range-m` arms are NOT cleanly paired (a cutoff permanently stops all RNG draws, diverging the stream per cutoff distance) — trust each arm's own n=6 aggregate, not cross-arm per-seed deltas. Dropout-rate arms ARE cleanly paired (identical draw sequence).
- **Decision:** report this envelope as the writeup's limitations number. Do NOT tune further guidance. Documented future lever (NOT built): `--coast-search` (bounded yaw-sweep reacquisition, ADR-0020) is the obvious mitigation against this exact cliff for non-maneuvering targets — a clean next experiment if the project resumes.
- **Date:** 2026-07-06. (Autonomous Gazebo stress sweep; worker ran sims, main session Fable wrote the ADR. Files: logs/mc_{dropout2x,dropout4x,jamcut8,jamcut12,jamcut16,jamcut20}_9ms.csv.)

## ADR-0032 — Portfolio demo video: hero flight captured, plus a real pre-existing race-condition bug found and worked around
- **Context:** finishing the portfolio demo video. Infra (world-name env-var fix, chase-camera sensor, `scripts/demo_capture_frames.py`, `scripts/render_hud.py`, `scripts/compose_demo.sh`) was already built and committed (ecc6568); remaining work was verifying the interceptor's FPV reskin, tightening the chase-camera framing, and capturing one clean intercept flight end-to-end.
- **`INTERCEPTOR_WORLD_NAME` env-var fix (from the prior session, gate-safety re-confirmed here):** `m2_detect.py`/`m4_target_mover.py`/`s2_cue_mock.py`'s hardcoded `WORLD_NAME = "apriltag"` gained an env-var override, default unchanged. Re-verified this session: `check_m2.sh` (which does not set the var) still PASSED against the plain `apriltag` world with numbers matching the pre-existing baseline (below) — the override is a true no-op for every existing gated caller.
- **Interceptor FPV reskin (`models/x500_base`, a resource-path shadow of PX4's own `x500_base` — ADR-0005 mechanism, material-only changes to 9 visuals: dark carbon-black body + electric-blue arm-tip accents): found and fixed a real bug, then verified number-safe.** The model FAILED TO SPAWN AT ALL on first boot — `models/x500_base/model.config`'s `<description>` prose contained unescaped literal `<material>`/`<visual>` substrings, which broke gz's XML parser (`XML_ERROR_MISMATCHED_ELEMENT`) and silently dropped the entire model (cascading into "at least one link" / frame-graph errors and every PX4 sensor showing "missing"). Root cause was NOT the GPU (no `dxgk` errors during that boot) — fixed by de-XML-ifying the prose (removed the angle brackets from the description text; `gz sdf --check` and `check_m2.sh` both re-verified clean after). **After the fix: `check_m2.sh` PASSED, `detection_rate=1.000`, `mean_err_norm=0.0858 m`** (baseline 0.0861 m — unchanged within noise; `logs/m2_detect_20260707T192233Z.csv`). Model geometry/collision/inertial/sensors are untouched (material-only, per the shadow's own header comment) — this result confirms that claim rather than just trusting it.
- **Chase-camera pose, re-derived from real trajectory data, not a guess.** The world file's two prior analytical poses both aimed at an assumed engagement point (~5,0,0.5) that turned out wrong — a real hero-flight CSV shows CPA/ENGAGE actually happens ~12 m further down the running-start corridor, around (6.3,-12.2,1.1)/(6.5,-12.2,0.5). Re-derived the pose from that real CPA region and iterated LIVE (via `gz service .../set_pose` on the static camera model — confirmed working, no reboot needed per pose iteration) against real captured frames: final pose `14.900 -7.612 3.388 0 0.262 -2.647` (~10 m out, broadside to the dash bearing, ~15° pitch) gives a level horizon with sky occupying the upper ~30% of frame and both drones clearly distinguishable at the CPA range. Confirmed working in the real hero flight's own chase footage.
- **A real, pre-existing race-condition bug found (not a new one introduced this session):** `worlds/apriltag_demo.sdf`'s target spawns at its world-file default pose `(5, 0, 0.5)` (same as the plain gated `apriltag.sdf` — not reskin-specific). `m4_target_mover.py`'s own "pre-warm" relocation to the real `--target-start` only fires once the mover subprocess spawns, well into `CUE_WAIT`/`DASH` — by which time the interceptor's always-on camera detection thread had already locked onto the nearby default-position board. When the mover then teleports the tag 30 m away, the tracker's alpha-beta filter doesn't know and coasts on its stale ~5 m estimate while `gt_range` diverges to 25-30 m, guaranteeing a BREAKOFF/abort. **Every gated/ADR-0028/0030/0031 number avoided this because `mc_batch.sh` always pre-places the tag via its own external `gz service` call before launching `m4_intercept.py`** — calling the script directly (as this task, and the module's own CLI docstring example, both do) has no such guard. Confirmed via CSV forensics on 2 failed attempts (seeds 17, 23), both showing the identical signature (`r_hat_m` frozen near 5 m while `gt_range` explodes). **Workaround applied (not a code fix):** an external tag pre-placement `gz service` call before launching `m4_intercept.py`, mirroring `mc_batch.sh`'s own pattern — 3rd attempt (seed 31) with this fix reached a clean intercept immediately. **Flagged, not fixed in code:** `m4_intercept.py` already knows `--target-start` and the world name; it should probably do this pre-placement internally rather than relying on every caller to replicate `mc_batch.sh`'s external step. Full forensic detail in `demo_out/README.md`.
- **Hero result:** `miss_distance_m=1.061, clean=1, engaged=1` (`logs/m4_intercept_pronav_20260707T194623Z.csv`, cue-seed 31, the ADR-0030 FIX config — running-start + velocity-emission cue + `--dash-unclamp`, realistic degraded cue, 9 m/s pronav — with `--early-handoff` retained). Consistent with ADR-0030/0031's published ~1.19 m mean for this exact config.
- **Chase/HUD sync gotcha (new, documented for reuse):** `scripts/demo_capture_frames.py` was started a few seconds before `m4_intercept.py` began logging (deliberate, to not miss takeoff); `compose_demo.sh`'s ffmpeg hstack pairs HUD and chase frames BY INDEX ONLY, with no time correction. Uncorrected, this desyncs the composite by the capture head-start (~4.7 s / ~141 frames here) — worst at the CPA moment, where the HUD would show "closest approach" while the chase pane was still mid-DASH. Fixed by trimming/renumbering the raw chase capture to start at the CSV's own `t_sim[0]` before compositing; verified by pulling a frame at the composite's CPA timestamp and confirming the HUD readout (`t=163.188s`, `RANGE 2.31m`) matches what the chase pane shows. Not yet automated — `compose_demo.sh`/`demo_capture_frames.py` should probably grow a `--sim-t-start` alignment step so this manual trim doesn't need repeating.
- **WSL2 GPU-wedge gotcha (re-confirmed, not re-triggered) + the fix:** a prior session's ~10 rapid boot/kill cycles wedged the WSL2 `/dev/dxg` GPU passthrough (`dxgkio_escape: Ioctl failed: -75` in `dmesg`, camera topics going permanently silent) — the documented fix is a host-side `wsl --shutdown` from Windows PowerShell (drops all WSL distros, coordinate before running), not fixable from inside the Linux guest. This session ran 6 total sim boots (over the ≤4 target, but each was deliberate: 2 for the reskin bug/verify, 1 for chase-cam pose tuning, 3 for flight attempts against the race-condition bug above) with a ≥30s cooldown and a post-boot `dmesg`+camera-topic check every time; every cluster of `dxgk` errors observed correlated with the PRIOR boot's teardown window (confirmed by timestamp), never a live boot's active window — the GPU never re-wedged this session. Discipline (cooldown + post-boot camera-publish check + immediate stop-and-report on any silent topic or new post-boot `dxgk` error) held up as the right operating procedure.
- **Date:** 2026-07-07.

- The demo was re-cut after this ADR was logged: the shipped assets (`demo_out/*`, per
  `demo_out/README.md`) are built from a later hero flight, **miss 0.632 m**

  1.061 m first capture recorded above. Both draws are consistent with the ~1.19 m

## ADR-0033 — Post-M5 roadmap RATIFIED (builder, 2026-07-07): protect the M5 finish line, then hardware bench → markerless seeker → EKF
- **Context:** M5 is ~90% done (Monte-Carlo n=48 ADR-0029; running-start Gazebo-confirmed ADR-0028 addendum; demo video shipped ADR-0032). Builder set explicit priorities for what comes next, with rationale aimed at how aerospace interviewers read a portfolio.
- **Decision (the ratified order):**
  0. **Finish M5 first — it's a multiplier, not a jump.** A 95%-done portfolio piece reads as "doesn't finish things." Remaining scope pinned (this ADR): (a) adopt P-8 corrected cue constants (σ_R c=4.45e-05, separate `--datum-bias-m` — ADR-0017; + the 0.20 s WORST latency tier — ADR-0016) into `s2_cue_mock` defaults and re-earn the S2 gate; (b) path suite — S-weave + jink velocity schedules and a west-approach geometry (the never-mirror-tested east/world_x axis + the L2R/R2L asymmetry second batch, by construction); (c) fix the ADR-0032 pre-placement race in `m4_intercept.py` itself (README reproduce instructions expose the footgun to outsiders); (d) larger-n final batch on the adopted deployment profile at speeds **6/9/12** (comparability with ADR-0029/0028/0030 — supersedes the older 6/8/10 note in NEXT.md); (e) plots (trajectory overlays, miss histogram/CDF, per-path, Pk-vs-radius) → README final numbers + GIF + reproduce instructions.
  1. **Touch real hardware — ADR-0012 Stage 0 bench (~$230: Pi 5 8GB ~$120 + accessories ~$35 + global-shutter cam + wide lens ~$75).** Interviewers systematically discount simulation; the bench turns "I simulated it" into "my perception stack runs on the real embedded target, with a measured sim-vs-bench gap." Software side is pre-staged (`frame_source.py`, pyapriltags import swap, `calibrate_camera.py`). **Builder action: order parts now** — lead time overlaps the M5 finish.
  2. **Kill the AprilTag (attack the #1 disclosed risk).** A classical-CV markerless seeker (motion/blob track of the target drone in Gazebo or on bench video) feeding the SAME bearing interface. Even a partial result converts the project from "guidance demo with a solved perception problem" into "seeker development," and completes the disclosed-risk arc. Design-as-ADR before building.
  3. **EKF target-track upgrade, A/B'd with the project's own discipline** (paired seeds n≥8, mechanism evidence). The Kalman filter is the most-asked GNC interview topic; even a null result vs alpha-beta at this noise level is a sophisticated, defensible answer (Kalata's Gazebo rejection, ADR-0013, is the precedent).
- **Why this order:** finish-line protection first (small, planned work); then highest interviewer-credibility-per-dollar (bench); then the intellectual centerpiece (seeker); the EKF is pure software and can interleave with 1–2.
- **Model-usage directive (builder, same message):** use Fable only for organization / TODO / cleanup / direction; hand execution to subagents immediately (the defense-sim terms flag Fable turns); if the session bounces to Opus mid-execution, keep working there.
- **Date:** 2026-07-07.

## ADR-0033 addendum (2026-07-08) — two scope corrections from the M5-finish build workflow
- **"West mirror" → `oblique_close` (accepted).** ADR-0026's literal negative-start_x mirror is PHYSICALLY MEANINGLESS in this setup: the tag board rigidly faces world −X, so a mirrored target shows its back to the interceptor and can never be detected. The thing the mirror test was FOR — exercising the never-tested east/world_x sign channel — is achieved instead by the new `--geometry oblique_close` arm: the first-ever nonzero target world-X velocity (dev-verified in Gazebo: vx=−4.243 m/s vs 0 in every prior arm, start_x stays positive, X floor 0.5 m). A sign error in the east channel of the frame math or guidance would now surface as a systematic miss on this arm. Honest naming retained (`oblique_close`, not "west mirror"). Caveat carried: its default x0 needs raising in batch arms (closing approach overshoots from the short default).
- **The P-8 "gate re-earn" scope was wrong in ADR-0033.** `check_s2.sh` flies the flat `--sigma 0.5` cue path, which is byte-identical after the constants adoption — the gate CANNOT exercise the corrected σ_R model (it never passes `--sigma-range`). The gate's role for this change is regression only (default path unchanged); the actual re-earn of numbers under the corrected constants happens in the M5 final batch's realistic-cue arms (`--sigma-range --datum-bias-m ... --dropout-markov`, the ADR-0030 config). Corollary: ADR-0030/0031's realistic-cue numbers were flown under the OLD ~180×-steeper σ_R curve — the final batch supersedes them and may come out better.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08.

## ADR-0034 — The post-AprilTag capstone: covariance-gated mid-course FUSION (EKF) is the path forward, and it's why the old fusion null must be RE-OPENED (builder direction, 2026-07-08)
- **Context / builder question:** as we remove the AprilTag (queue item 2, markerless seeker), the terminal camera loses its clean data — sub-degree bearing / ~5% range become box-based ~1–2° / 15–30%. The builder asked: isn't cross-platform fusion (ground stereo cue + onboard camera) then *needed*, run through a Kalman filter, with the ability to still fall back to camera-only when the ground track looks worse? **Answer: yes — this is the correct forward path, and it unifies the three separate post-M5 threads (seeker + EKF + fusion) into one capstone experiment. Decide-and-log; full spec goes into the seeker + EKF briefs and a dedicated fusion ADR when built (gated behind the M5 finish).**
- **Why the ADR-0018-addendum fusion NULL does not settle this (the key insight):** `--fuse-midcourse` was measured default-OFF under the two conditions that *structurally suppress fusion's value* — (1) a CLEAN AprilTag terminal seeker (camera already excellent → a noisier cue can only dilute it), and (2) the FIXED-GAIN alpha-beta tracker (blends sources by a hand-set bearing-weighted rule; cannot weight by each source's live uncertainty). Flip BOTH — markerless (noisier camera, so the cue again carries information) + an EKF (weights by covariance) — and the conclusion can legitimately flip. The prior null is the *reason to re-run*, not a reason to skip; it was measured where fusion couldn't win.
- **The elegant part (and the answer to "fall back to camera if ground is worse"):** that fallback is NOT a bolt-on gate — it is what a Kalman filter does NATIVELY. Give the EKF both measurements with honest per-source noise models and (a) **covariance weighting** auto-drives the ground cue's weight toward zero when its uncertainty blows up (far range, datum bias, stale/dropped link) so the estimate rides the camera; (b) the **innovation (chi-square) gate** rejects an individual ground measurement outright when it disagrees with the prediction beyond threshold — a literal "this ground reading looks wrong, ignore it." Adaptive fusion falls out of the estimator; you don't hand-code the if-statement. Strong interview point.
- **Where the value most likely IS and IS NOT (honest, per ADR-0023/0030):** the kinematic ceiling (ADR-0023) still caps the *terminal* miss — a better track won't out-run time-to-go at 12 m/s from a bad geometry. So fusion+EKF's expected payoff is **mid-course robustness** — earlier/warmer lock and FEWER DASH-ABORTS — not the final meters. That is exactly the binding failure ADR-0030 found under a degraded cue (33% of 9 m/s flights never reached handoff). Targeting *handoff-reach rate* and mid-course track RMSE is a stronger, more honest headline than chasing terminal miss.
- **Honesty boundary — non-negotiable (this is the whole project's thesis):** the headline capability is the comms-DENIED terminal intercept, so fusion stays **MID-COURSE ONLY** (`--fuse-midcourse`'s existing scope) and the terminal MUST still degrade to camera-only. A real audit-design subtlety to build: the `cue_reads_post_handoff=0` audit must EXTEND to "no cue-tainted EKF state OR covariance survives the handoff," or the jam-resistance claim quietly breaks through the filter's memory. Warm handoff (seeker tracking through the dash, cross-validated against the cue, before the link is cut) is legitimate and desired; sustained simultaneous fusion into the terminal is not.
- **Experiment shape (focused ladder, not the full cross):** the axes are fusion {off, on} × tracker {alpha-beta, EKF} × seeker {AprilTag, markerless}. Don't run all 8 cells; ladder up from the current baseline. Metrics: handoff-reach/latch rate (the ADR-0030 failure mode), mid-course track RMSE vs `gt_*` (scoring-only), LOS-rate error, and end-to-end miss — with the paired-seed n≥8 + "not significant at this n" discipline. Pre-register SUCCESS vs NULL criteria so the conclusion can't be post-hoc.
- **Sequencing:** behind the M5 finish (protected line, ADR-0033 item 0). This is the CAPSTONE that combines queue items 2 (markerless seeker) and 3 (EKF) rather than a fourth separate thread — build the seeker and the EKF, then this fusion experiment is where they meet and where the AprilTag removal's cost is measured and (partly) bought back.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Fork-worker Q&A with the builder during the M5-finish session; logged as direction, not yet built or gated.)

## ADR-0035 — Bird-vs-hostile-UAS discrimination + CONOPS/ROE doctrine — layered, red-team-hardened, ROE-tunable operating point, defaults VETO-ALL until benched (builder mandate 2026-07-08)
- **Status:** DIRECTION to pursue — **NOT ratifiable and NOT buildable as written.** Adopted as the design direction + doctrine; blocked from ratification/build until (i) the eight red-team fixes (§0 of `docs/bird_discrimination_design.md`) are implemented and (ii) the Stage-0 bench produces `P_correct(R)` and sets thresholds. Until then all operating-point thresholds are UNSET and **the interlock DEFAULTS TO VETO-ALL (engages nothing).** Gated behind M5 + the markerless seeker/classifier existing + the bench (ADR-0033 item 2, ADR-0034). Full design: `docs/bird_discrimination_design.md`.
- **Context.** Removing the AprilTag (ADR-0033 item 2) forfeits the fiducial's implicit "this IS the target" guarantee, and a real sky has birds — the #1 fielded-C-UAS false-alarm source (ADR-0013; ADR-0019 §3). Safety + ethics require **affirmative positive-ID of a hostile UAS before any commit** and **fail-safe (break off) on doubt**; the threat model denies the datalink in the terminal, so bird rejection cannot depend on the link. Single-frame appearance is insufficient at acquisition range (drone and bird both ~10×10 px; best published FP 3.73% on *resolved* crops, worse at few-pixel range). The measured camera-only terminal window is **0.41 s median** (ADR-0023) — it dictates veto timing. An adversarial red-team found the three-phase *direction* sound but the original *mechanization* fail-UNSAFE on terminal-veto timing, confidence-signal semantics, and streak/track binding. Builder doctrine (2026-07-08): the asymmetric bird-vs-hostile cost is real but **finite and context-dependent, not infinite** — a system so bird-cautious it lets an armed hostile reach the people it defends is *also* a failure.
- **Options.** (a) onboard single-frame classifier gates engage — REJECTED (FP too high at few-pixel range, decision made where sensing is weakest); (b) ground rig decides and streams the label into the terminal — REJECTED (violates the comms-denied thesis); (c) **confirm mid-course (multi-sensor), carry a committed decision, hold with a fast onboard veto** — ADOPTED, hardened + doctrine-wrapped. On the cost model: a single fixed ultra-conservative constant — REJECTED (a system that can never risk a bird can let a real hostile through); replaced with an ROE-tunable operating point inside a fixed fail-safe envelope.
- **Decision.**
  1. **Layered architecture.** A decision-level channel (Bayesian log-likelihood-ratio-sum default) fuses onboard appearance + ground kinematics + ground stereo TRUE SIZE + optional radar micro-Doppler into a **dedicated hostile-class posterior `P(hostile)`** — a NEW field, never the `decision_margin` detection scalar. **Ground-stereo is the PRIMARY discriminator** (manufactures PID before commit via absolute true-size, the kinematic time-series, mid-course time, optional micro-Doppler — none available in the 0.41 s onboard terminal); the onboard seeker **carries the committed decision + fast veto**, never originates classification from cold.
  2. **Red-team hardening (eight fixes, all fail toward break-off):** fast ASYMMETRIC terminal veto (positive-ID-to-hold, not streak-to-abort, on the 0.41 s clock); interlock reads `P(hostile)` never `decision_margin`; hostile streak bound to a tracked OBJECT IDENTITY (resets on re-association/gap; terminal re-assoc = mandatory break-off); carried mid-course prior is VETO-ONLY/asymmetric; ARM requires an AFFIRMATIVE powered-drone signature (never "failed to look bird-like"); the sim gate proves PLUMBING only / UPPER BOUND; stale/dead/absent classifier == `P(hostile)`=0 == break-off (liveness watchdog); flocks/contested associations ⇒ break-off. ARM = added AND-clauses on the existing M4 state machine, **no new lethal path.**
  3. **CONOPS / ROE doctrine (§1.5).** The asymmetric cost is real but **finite/context-dependent.** Replace any hard-coded ultra-conservative threshold with a **TUNABLE operating point on the Pk-vs-false-engage ROC, set by ROE / deployment context** — maximally conservative (≈0) in peacetime/testing, shifted toward engagement when actively defending people against a confirmed threat. ROE sets **only** the acceptable false-positive *bound* (*where* on a *measured* ROC), and can **never** switch off the three invariants: affirmative PID always required, ambiguity always breaks off, pre-bench ⇒ VETO-ALL. This is **NOT "engage on doubt."**
  4. **Operational mitigations, first-class defense-in-depth:** (a) **range clearance** — geographic + temporal bird deconfliction during real testing so a classifier miss can't harm wildlife during bring-up; (b) **human-on-the-loop with manual abort authority** — a human can override/abort a running engagement and, at low autonomy, must positively authorize the commit. **Bring-up protocol (builder): test with no birds present, be ready to abort manually if it acts wrong.**
  5. **Comms-denied classification-honesty boundary:** a two-clause `classification_reads_post_handoff = 0` audit (extends ADR-0034) — post-handoff the engage traces to onboard evidence, and the carried ground prior is VETO-ONLY (may raise suspicion / lengthen confirmation / abort, never shorten N or lower the commit bar). Radar is confirm-only, never a dependency.
- **Why.** PID must be manufactured where the strong physics-based ground channels are live (true-size vs large birds, micro-Doppler, affirmative powered-flight kinematics) and *carried veto-only* into a 0.41 s terminal the onboard finishes alone; the fast asymmetric veto keeps the engage reversible on the terminal's own timescale. The asymmetric cost fixes the *default* operating point at high precision and makes every ambiguity fail toward break-off — but because the cost is finite, the operating point is an ROE parameter sliding *inside* a fixed fail-safe envelope, backed by operational defense-in-depth so no single fragile classifier must be perfect. Layered defense (interlock + operational controls + human abort) is the answer to the honest residual (jamming removes ground confirm at the terminal; small 0.3–0.5 m birds defeat true-size). Turns the project's #1 named failure mode into a design whose safety holes were found and closed before build.
- **Honesty.** NOT ratifiable/buildable as written — a DIRECTION + doctrine only, blocked until the eight fixes + the bench. All thresholds UNSET; interlock DEFAULTS TO VETO-ALL until the bench measures them; they MUST NOT be back-fit from the sim ROC (diagnostic/expository only). The sim gate proves PLUMBING only and is an UPPER BOUND — a green `check_bird_reject` is "wiring validated," NOT "bird rejection validated" (that is a BENCH claim: WORST-tier flapping/matched-footprint decoy + real-sky `P_correct(R)`). The ROE knob is bounded — the acceptable FP bound only, never removing affirmative PID or fail-safe break-off. Headline metric: false-engage-on-birds ≈ 0 is the *peacetime/testing* target; the fielded operating point is ROE-set within the fail-safe envelope; drone recall is secondary and may be < 100%. Open: small-bird residual, kinematic speed overlap (~10–25 m/s), cross-domain classifier haircut, BirDrone/WOSDETC licenses (§7).
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Builder safety mandate + CONOPS doctrine; research + synthesis + adversarial red-team + doctrine passes under ultracode; main session logged the ADR.)

## ADR-0036 — M5 FINAL Monte-Carlo (n=96): the adopted deployment profile makes the whole FPV band catchable — Pk@2.5 m 27%→100%; laws stay tied; maneuvers survive; east-axis sign channel clean
- **Context.** The M5 final batch on the ADOPTED profile — running start (`--y0-mag 29.3 --dash-speed 16 --dash-unclamp`) + velocity-emission cue (`--emit-velocity --cue-velocity`) + `--early-handoff`, under the REALISTIC degraded cue (`--sigma-range --datum-bias-m 0.5 --latency-jitter-s 0.05 --dropout-markov`) with the **P-8 corrected σ_R constants** (c=4.45e-05, ADR-0017) now default. 6 arms × 16 flights = **96 flights**, master-seed 42, idle load, per-arm CSV checkpoint (`logs/mc_final_{line6,line9,line12,weave9,jink9,oblique6}.csv`, merged `logs/mc_final_all.csv`; plots `plots/{pk_vs_radius_by_arm,miss_hist_cdf,per_path,traj_overlay}_20260708T040703Z.png`). **This SUPERSEDES ADR-0029's regime-map numbers (old hover geometry) and ADR-0030/0031's realistic-cue numbers (flown under the OLD ~180×-steeper σ_R curve).**
- **Headline (the M5 result).** 96.9% clean (93/96), mean miss **1.08 m**, median **0.93 m**. **Pk vs lethal radius (pooled, context): 0.50 m 9% · 1.0 m 53% · 1.5 m (net) 79% · 2.0 m 92% · 2.5 m 100%.** Against ADR-0029's old hover geometry (Pk@2.5 m = 27%, 12 m/s uncatchable 0/8) this is a transformation — the adopted deployment profile makes the **entire 6/9/12 m/s FPV band catchable.** Per-speed (ADR-0025, never pooled): 6 m/s pronav Pk@1.5 m 96%; 9 m/s pronav Pk@1.5 m 75%, Pk@2.0 m 88%; 12 m/s pronav Pk@1.5 m 38% but Pk@2.0 m 100% — 12 m/s is now caught, just needs the net (1.5 m) rather than ram (0.5 m) radius.
- **Guidance law: TIED on every path** (all per-path pursuit-vs-pronav deltas < the ~1 m run-to-run noise, n=4–8/cell). Reconfirms the ADR-0023/0029 kinematic-regime finding at FPV speed — now at ~1 m instead of ~3.6 m. Pro-nav's decisive 4.6–7.6× win remains the SLOWER-target M4 result (2 m/s, gated). The pair maps the regime: law dominates where there's time-to-go; geometry (running start) + kill radius dominate at FPV speed.
- **Maneuvers survive (S3/S4 folded in).** jink@9 (pronav 1.02 m) ≈ line@9 (1.08 m) — sharp discrete jinks are CHEAP. weave@9 is the worst maneuver (pronav mean 1.41 m) and shows a strong DIRECTIONAL split (L2R 2.18 m vs R2L 0.64 m). The kinematic/law-tie story holds under maneuver; the miss rises modestly and only the sustained-lateral-offset weave stresses it.
- **East/world_x sign channel CLEAN (the never-tested axis).** oblique_close@6 (first-ever nonzero target world-X velocity, vx≈−4.24 m/s) intercepts cleanly both ways: L2R 0.63 m, R2L 0.38 m, 16/16 clean+handoff — **no coordinate/sign bug on the mirror-untested east axis.** A reproducible **L2R-misses-more asymmetry** shows across BOTH geometries (oblique +0.25 m; weave +1.54 m), consistent with the fixed-tag-aspect perception effect flagged in ADR-0026/0027 (the board faces world −X, so the two approaches present different tag aspects) — a real perception asymmetry, not a guidance sign error (both directions close cleanly).
- **Honesty.** n=8/cell (16 with both directions); per-cell Pk CIs are wide and the pursuit-vs-pronav deltas sit within noise (reported tied, not ranked). Still the clean AprilTag sensor — this is the sim's honest UPPER BOUND; the markerless seeker (ADR-0033 item 2) regresses acquisition range and is the real cost of removing the tag. The running-start magnitude leans on the ground cue emitting a good velocity track (a real-system REQUIREMENT, ADR-0030). The weave directional split and the L2R/R2L asymmetry each warrant a confirmation batch if headlined tightly.
- **Decision.** ADOPT these as the M5 headline numbers (supersede ADR-0029/0030/0031 as noted). Portfolio framing: the M4 pro-nav-vs-pursuit win (gated, 2 m/s) AND this M5 regime map (running-start makes the FPV band catchable; laws tie; maneuvers survive; Pk@net-radius 79%) — the pair characterizes a SYSTEM.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Autonomous M5 final batch under ultracode; main session gated each arm + analyzed + logged. `mc_analyze.py` per-arm/paired output.)

## ADR-0037 — EKF vs alpha-beta A/B: NULL on miss AND track RMSE (as pre-registered) — but the paired clean-rate exposed a terminal-hold REGRESSION at the default Q; keep alpha-beta
- **Context.** Post-M5 queue item 3 (ADR-0033). Built a Cartesian-CV EKF (`scripts/ekf_tracker.py`, behind `--tracker {alphabeta,ekf}`, byte-identical alpha-beta default) per `docs/ekf_design_brief.md`, then ran the pre-registered paired-seed A/B: pronav / 9 m/s / line / standard / both-directions / n=8, master-seed 42 (identical cue seeds per run_idx — the ONLY difference is the tracker), adopted running-start profile + realistic corrected cue. Analysis: `scripts/ekf_ab_analyze.py` (paired deltas + 95% CI on miss, track RMSE, coverage, LOS-rate; gt_* scoring-only). Arms `logs/mc_ekfab_{alphabeta,ekf}.csv`.
- **PRIMARY (miss): NULL, exactly as pre-registered.** alpha-beta 1.020 m (sd 0.085) vs EKF 1.011 m (sd 0.212); paired Δ(ekf−ab) = **−0.009 m, 95% CI [−0.127, +0.117]** → tied (CI straddles 0). The miss is kinematically limited (ADR-0023), not estimator-limited — a better filter does not move it, as the brief predicted.
- **SECONDARY (track quality): also NULL.** Every paired delta straddles 0: range-track RMSE Δ +0.076 m [−0.198, +0.351]; position-track RMSE Δ +0.021 m [−0.039, +0.081] (n≈780 ticks/arm, the highest-mass metric); terminal coverage Δ −0.008 [−0.100, +0.083]; LOS-rate proxy thin (n=6, not significant). The EKF did NOT produce a cleaner track here.
- **THE ACTUAL FINDING — a clean-rate REGRESSION the miss metric HID.** alpha-beta clean **8/8**; EKF clean **2/8** (handoff-reach 100% both). The 6 non-clean EKF flights ABORTED with "lost tag >5.0 s (far from target)" — a severe terminal-track-loss — whereas alpha-beta's 8/8 gracefully reached the normal terminal-dropout coast. The min-range MISS is tied only because it is recorded even on an abort (the coast still passes near the target), so **the headline miss metric masks an operational terminal-hold regression that the paired clean-rate exposes** — the value of A/B-ing more than one metric. Cause: almost certainly the shipped **Q_ACCEL_PSD=64 (worst-credible, UNTUNED — flagged in the build review)**: a high process-noise EKF widens its covariance and trusts noisy near-CPA measurements, losing lock where lambda_dot spikes and detections thin. A Q-tuning sweep could plausibly recover parity — but the miss is kinematically capped regardless (won't improve), so the return is low.
- **Decision: KEEP alpha-beta as the default tracker.** A reproducible NEGATIVE result — the EKF does not beat alpha-beta on miss or track RMSE and at default tuning regresses the operational clean-rate — reinforcing the ADR-0013 Kalata rejection and the ADR-0023 kinematic diagnosis. `--tracker ekf` stays as a documented, default-OFF option; the `ekf_tracker.py` machinery is sound (10/10 unit tests, NIS/NEES consistent). **Honest portfolio framing (the sophisticated answer):** "I implemented an EKF, A/B'd it with paired seeds, got the pre-registered null on miss AND track quality — and the paired clean-rate caught that at default tuning it actually lost the tag terminally more often, which I traced to the untuned process noise. Since the miss is kinematically capped, the simpler alpha-beta wins." The most-asked GNC interview topic, answered with a measured result.
- **Honesty / follow-ups.** n=8/cell — the miss/RMSE nulls are "not significant at this n," the clean-rate delta (8/8 vs 2/8) is a large, likely-real swing but on a single paired batch. Optional low-value follow-ups: a Q-tuning sweep to test EKF-parity; the WORST-latency cue tier (the brief's discriminating cell); the `--fuse-midcourse` + EKF capstone (ADR-0034) where covariance-weighting has an actual dual-source stream to exploit (the one place the EKF is still expected to earn its keep). `--tracker ekf` governs the engage loop only, not `run_bench`.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (EKF build + adversarial review + paired Gazebo A/B under ultracode; main session gated the arms, analyzed, logged.)
- **Addendum (2026-07-08 later session, ADR-0041 P0 forensics): the clean-rate regression was a POST-CPA SCORING ARTIFACT, not an EKF flight defect.** Offline forensics (`scripts/ekf_lockout_forensics.py`, a perfect 6/6-vs-0/10 discriminator) + the arm CSVs: all 6 "aborted" EKF flights intercepted at normal quality (miss 0.675–1.339 m, paired mean 1.011 vs alpha-beta's 1.020; 3 of 6 BEAT their paired twin) — the aborts fired ~5 s AFTER the pass because the EKF's physically-CORRECT post-CPA range extrapolation (separation really grows; gt confirms) crossed the `r_hat >= TERMINAL_RANGE_M=3.0` branch into the `LOST_TAG_ABORT_S=5.0` abort clock, while alpha-beta's physically-IMPOSSIBLE negative coasted range (−12..−21 m) stayed `< 3.0` and rode the terminal-hold branch forever. The Q-tune replay screen (`scripts/ekf_q_replay.py`) independently found NO over-wide-P signature at Q=64 (the cause line above inferred, never measured): pooled gate rejections 12.5% (tight-P side), lower Q monotonically WORSE, max consecutive rejections 2 anywhere (no lockout cascade either). This addendum does NOT flip the decision by itself (alpha-beta stays default; the miss/RMSE nulls stand) but it WITHDRAWS the "traced to the untuned process noise" clause — the trace was to abort-branch bookkeeping — and removes the evidence that the EKF is flight-unworthy. Fix path (deferred to fusion P0, ADR-0041): analyzer-level post-CPA abort reclassification first (zero flight-code change), branch-logic redesign only if warranted; and re-examine the markerless arms' aborts through the same pre/post-CPA lens before trusting their clean-rates.

## ADR-0038 — Markerless seeker live A/B (Option A): killing the AprilTag costs ~+1 m median miss + ~25 pp clean-rate, driven by sparse detection, not guidance
- **Context.** Post-M5 roadmap Option A (builder ratified 2026-07-08): fly the tag-less two-stage markerless seeker (`--seeker markerless`, ADR-0033 item 2) under real ego-motion and measure the cost vs the AprilTag, paired. Infrastructure built this session: combined venv (`.venv-seeker` + gz/mavsdk bridge, main `.venv` untouched), `worlds/markerless.sdf` (tag-less body `fpv_target_markerless`), `INTERCEPTOR_TARGET_MODEL` entity-name parametrization (fixed 3 hardcodes that broke gt-scoring + cue synthesis on the markerless world), and `mc_batch.sh` `MC_WORLD/MC_TARGET_MODEL/MC_SEEKER/MC_VENV_PYTHON` knobs (defaults keep the apriltag path byte-identical).
- **Method.** Paired n=8 (master-seed 42, cue_seeds identical across arms), the adopted M5 deployment profile (running start `--y0-mag 29.3 --x0 6.5`, `--dash-speed 16 --early-handoff --cue-velocity --dash-unclamp`, realistic degraded cue), pronav / line / 9 m/s / both directions, both arms flown under `.venv-seeker`. Seeker = default COCO YOLOv8n two-stage (AGPL, the off-the-shelf `markerless_loop.py` path). `logs/mc_ab_{apriltag,markerless}.csv`.
- **Result (paired, 8/8 seeds).**
  - **AprilTag control: 8/8 clean, 8/8 handoff, median miss 0.92 m** (mean 1.01), Pk@1.5 m 8/8, Pk@2.5 m 8/8, first-detection median 5.3 m. This REPRODUCES ADR-0036's M5 line-9 numbers → the `mc_batch.sh` MC_* edits are byte-safe (regression check PASS).
  - **Markerless: 6/8 clean, 8/8 handoff, median miss 1.83 m** (mean 3.39, dragged by 2 outliers), Pk@1.5 m 4/8, Pk@2.5 m 5/8, first-detection median 1.3 m.
  - **Paired miss delta (markerless − apriltag): median +1.03 m, mean +2.38 m.**
- **The mechanism (the honest story).** Markerless MATCHES the AprilTag when it acquires early (seed 26226: first-det 18.3 m → 1.06 vs 0.92 m; 91162: 1.13 vs 1.08; 229259: 0.98 vs 0.92). The whole degradation is **2 late-acquisition flybys** (256788: first-det 1.2 m → 10.0 m miss; 776647: 0.9 m → 6.9 m) caused by SPARSE terminal coverage (5–24 % vs the tag's near-continuous ~14 Hz): when the seeker doesn't lock until <1.5 m, there is no terminal window left to correct and the interceptor flies past. Consistent with the ADR-0023 kinematic ceiling — miss among the flights that acquire in time stays ~1–1.5 m; what regresses is the RELIABILITY of acquiring in time, i.e. clean-rate, not the accuracy of the intercepts that complete.
- **A real acquisition finding.** The static range probe (`probe_markerless_range.py`) measured only ~2 m acquisition on the tag-less body, but IN FLIGHT the seeker acquires at 8–18 m — because the two-stage's cheap **motion-proposal** layer needs a MOVING target the static teleport probe never provided. So "lab (static) ranks, Gazebo (flight) decides" applied to the seeker too: the static number under-priced in-flight acquisition.
- **Decision.** Markerless intercept is VIABLE on the deployment profile (75 % clean, 100 % handoff) with an off-the-shelf detector — a real "killed the AprilTag" result — but at a measured reliability cost the fix for is (a) Option B, an in-domain fine-tune to densify detection, and (b) Option C, extending ground-cue custody (see ADR-0039). Keep `--seeker apriltag` as the default; `--seeker markerless` is the disclosed, measured alternative.
- **Honesty (re-earned for the new guidance path).** `cue_reads_post_handoff=0` on all 8 flights; `tests/test_honesty_static.py` 7/7 PASS (command chain clean after the entity-name edits); the seeker reads only camera pixels + fixed intrinsics in `detect()` (its `gt_*` refs are in the offline `--eval` path only). Off-the-shelf weights are AGPL (COCO YOLOv8n) — a license note for the public artifact; the deployable answer is a permissive fine-tune (Option B) or the MIT model on real hardware.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Combined-venv + markerless-world infra, entity-name fix, mc_batch parametrization, paired A/B; overnight autonomous, Opus.)

## ADR-0039 — Ground-rig division-of-labor sweep (Option C): handoff-TIMING levers do NOT recover the markerless regression — it is a detection-coverage problem, and the tight-ceiling lever is context-dependent by seeker
- **Context.** Option C (builder ratified 2026-07-08), executed per `docs/ground_rig_sweep_plan.md`. Question: can letting the ground-rig cue carry the track longer (handoff-timing knobs) buy back the markerless clean-rate regression (ADR-0038), WITHOUT moving the kinematically-capped terminal miss (ADR-0023, pre-registered H3)? The plan's venv-gap prerequisite is solved by ADR-0038's combined venv + `mc_batch` knobs.
- **Method.** Six arms, all n=8 paired (master-seed 42, adopted deployment profile, pronav/line/9 m/s). BASE = ADR-0038 apriltag control; Arm A = ADR-0038 markerless (default: `--early-handoff`, handoff-range 10 m). New arms: **B** markerless, drop `--early-handoff` (stricter 3-streak); **C** markerless, `--handoff-range 20` (raised ceiling, pre-registered EXPECTED null); **D** markerless, `--handoff-range 2.5` (tight ceiling); **E** apriltag, `--handoff-range 2.5` (lever isolation). `logs/mc_grsweep_arm{B,C,D,E}_*.csv`.
- **Result (clean-rate / median miss / Pk@2.5 m / dash-aborts).**
  - BASE apriltag: **8/8**, 0.92 m, 8/8, 0.
  - Arm A markerless default: 6/8, 1.83 m, 5/8, 0.
  - Arm B markerless stricter-streak: **5/8, 2.61 m, 4/8**, 0 — WORSE, not better.
  - Arm C markerless wide-ceiling(20): **4/8, 5.71 m, 2/8**, 0 — WORSE (not the predicted null): a raised ceiling lets NOISY long-range detections trigger a premature handoff to camera-only where the track can't be sustained.
  - Arm D markerless tight-ceiling(2.5): 4/8, **1.84 m**, 5/8, 0 — miss stays competitive (forces the cue to carry until close, so the delivered ZEM is good when it does hand off), but clean-rate is NOT recovered.
  - Arm E apriltag tight-ceiling(2.5): 5/8 clean, **handoff 5/8, 3 dash-aborts**, 1.01 m — the tight ceiling HURTS the AprilTag (it normally acquires at 9–12 m; a 2.5 m ceiling starves the detection streak so the dash overshoots and aborts).
- **Findings.**
  1. **H2 is NULL/NEGATIVE.** No handoff-timing lever recovers the markerless clean-rate; the stricter-streak and wide-ceiling levers actively HURT. Per the plan's pre-registered §5, this means the markerless regression is **a raw detection-probability/coverage problem, not a handoff-timing problem** — redirecting the fix to the seeker itself (Option B fine-tune) and sharpening the case for the ADR-0034 fusion capstone (a covariance-weighted EKF buys CONTINUOUS track quality through the window, not a single threshold trigger).
  2. **The tight-ceiling lever is context-dependent (opposite sign by seeker)** — Arm E vs Arm D on the identical flag: it starves the AprilTag's far-acquisition streak (3 dash-aborts) while being tolerable for the markerless seeker (which only acquires near anyway). A clean "the same mechanism helps or hurts depending on the sensor's acquisition envelope" result.
  3. **H3 (kinematic ceiling) HOLDS.** Among flights that reach a clean terminal, median miss stays ~1–2 m across every arm; what the levers move is clean-RATE (completion), never the accuracy of completed intercepts — exactly ADR-0023's prediction that a smarter/later handoff cannot out-run a delivered-ZEM problem.
- **Decision.** Do not adopt any handoff-timing change for the markerless seeker (none helps). The markerless regression is owned by the seeker's detection coverage → Option B (in-domain fine-tune) is the correct next lever; the fusion capstone (ADR-0034) remains the structural answer. Keep `--early-handoff` + default 10 m ceiling for both seekers.
- **Honesty.** `cue_reads_post_handoff=0` across all arms; same camera-only-terminal boundary as every prior gate (no EKF/fusion in play, so ADR-0034's extended "no cue-tainted covariance survives handoff" audit is not yet owed). n=8/arm: the clean-rate swings (8/8 → 4–6/8) are meaningful directional signal on a single paired batch, not tight significance; framed as "not significant at this n" where deltas are sub-noise.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Overnight sweep driver, 40 flights, GPU-wedge-guarded; per `docs/ground_rig_sweep_plan.md`; Opus.)

## ADR-0040 — In-domain fine-tune (Option B): closes the markerless acquisition-range regression (static 2/8 -> 8/8, mAP50 0.992) and improves the flight aggregate, but a positives-only full-frame model is necessary-not-sufficient for flight clean-rate
- **Context.** ADR-0038/0039 localized the markerless regression to detection COVERAGE (the off-the-shelf COCO two-stage acquires only terminally, ~2 m static; no handoff-timing lever recovers it). Option B (builder ratified 2026-07-08; "for real life we can use the MIT model" -- the sim fine-tune is a demo/sim-domain aid): fine-tune a single-class nano on IN-DOMAIN Gazebo renders to densify detection.
- **Method.** Rendered 495 frames of the tag-less body across a range x lateral x height x yaw grid (`render_sim_dataset.py`, PIXEL-EXACT gt-projected labels via m2_detect.PoseTracker -- gt used at TRAINING time only, the same scoring-only boundary as a human labeler). Fine-tuned YOLO11n (CPU, `.venv-seeker-train`) -> **mAP50 0.992 by epoch 6** (easy domain: dark quad on gray). Exported ONNX @640; run FULL-FRAME via `FinetunedNNSeeker` (onnxruntime, single-class decode) selected by `MARKERLESS_NN_WEIGHTS`. Paired n=8 A/B (master-seed 42) vs the ADR-0038 off-the-shelf markerless + AprilTag arms.
- **Forensic fix (documented, load-bearing).** The FIRST tuned arm was WORSE (seed 26226 miss 1.06 -> 3.10 m). Forensics: the positives-only fine-tune, run full-frame with NO self-mask, FALSE-LOCKED on the interceptor's own body-fixed prop arms at the FOV periphery -- detections clustered at bearing -44/+33 deg with a constant ~1.5 m range while gt_range was 29 m (meas/gt 0.07), the confident-wrong-bearing hazard that corrupts the alpha-beta LOS filter (seeker_prototype_results.md s3.4, the exact failure the two-stage's self-mask was built to kill). Added the interior/self-mask gate to `FinetunedNNSeeker` (reject |bearing| > 30 deg or a box touching the L/R frame edge). Post-fix flight 0: 1.15 m, zero |bearing|>30 deg locks. Static probe still 8/8.
- **Result (static acquisition, then paired flight A/B).**
  - **Static probe: 2/8 -> 8/8 ranges fire (2-12 m) at 0.87-0.99 conf** -- the acquisition-range regression is CLOSED on the static body.
  - **Flight coverage median 0.11 -> 0.25** (doubled -- dense detection as designed).
  - **Mean miss 3.39 -> 2.45 m**: the fine-tune ELIMINATED both off-the-shelf catastrophic flybys (seed 256788 10.04 -> 2.50 m clean; 776647 6.94 -> 1.35 m clean).
  - **Median gap to the AprilTag HALVED: +1.03 m -> +0.66 m.**
  - **BUT clean-rate is a WASH: 6/8 both.** The fine-tune fixed 2 off-the-shelf flybys but BROKE 2 it should have kept (772247 1.46 -> 4.35 m abort; 33327 2.19 -> 5.95 m abort) -- residual near-boresight false-positives + a range-estimate bias (the fine-tuned boxes read LARGE, so known-size range underestimates ~15x; tolerated only because bearing is the primary guidance input).
- **Decision.** The in-domain fine-tune CLOSES the acquisition-range regression that ADR-0038/0039 identified as the root cause, and improves the flight aggregate (mean miss, worst-case flybys, coverage, median gap-to-tag) -- but a positives-only, full-frame, uncalibrated-range model is **necessary-not-sufficient to lift flight CLEAN-RATE**. Keep the off-the-shelf two-stage as the `--seeker markerless` default; the fine-tuned path is `MARKERLESS_NN_WEIGHTS` opt-in. **v2 follow-up (clear next levers):** (1) hard NEGATIVE training frames (own-airframe / background-only) to kill the residual false-positives; (2) a calibrated known-size range (or fuse the two-stage crop-verify); (3) higher train imgsz for smaller-target range. This also directly feeds the ADR-0034 fusion capstone (a covariance-gated EKF would down-weight the biased-range/low-confidence markerless measurements automatically).
- **Honesty.** `cue_reads_post_handoff=0` on all 8 tuned flights; `tests/test_honesty_static.py` 7/7; the live detector reads pixels only (labels were gt-projected offline at training time). License: the YOLO11n base is AGPL (ultralytics tooling) -- fine for a sim/demo artifact, but the deployable answer is an Apache-2.0 nano or the MIT model on real hardware (the whole point of "for real life we use the MIT model"). GPU flat all session (dxgk ~4076-4095, ~200 boots, zero wedges).
- **Addendum (v2 diagnosis, same session).** The 2 tuned flights that regressed vs off-the-shelf (seeds 772247, 33327) both aborted "lost detection >1 s inside 5 m" -- a terminal DROPOUT of the real target, not an overshoot. And the range bias persists even for near-boresight (|bearing|<30 deg) detections (meas/gt median 0.06, n=506 ticks) -- i.e. the positives-only model still OVER-FIRES on own-airframe / oversized boxes even INSIDE the self-mask gate, occasionally displacing the real-target lock. So the PRIMARY v2 lever is hard NEGATIVES (own-airframe *flight* frames -- the static ground render doesn't show the props, so this needs a flight-frame capture pass), with range-calibration strictly SECONDARY: a range fix cannot help when the detections themselves are wrong, only the formula would be. This sharpens the v2 direction above.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Render + CPU fine-tune + ONNX export + FinetunedNNSeeker + self-mask forensic fix + paired A/B; overnight autonomous, Opus.)

## ADR-0041 — Fusion capstone execution design ratified (council-reviewed); two pre-build failure modes found by executing the EKF offline; per-tick batch honesty auditor built and the ADR-0038/0040 arms retro-audited
- **Context.** ADR-0034 required design-as-ADR before building the covariance-gated mid-course fusion. Draft design written 2026-07-08 (`docs/fusion_capstone_design.md`), then reviewed by a 3-member council (estimation theory / honesty boundary / experiment design). Two members backed findings with executed experiments (running `ekf_tracker.py` at the project's own ADR-0017 constants; measuring flight costs from batch logs) rather than prose review.
- **Council findings that changed the design (full detail in the design doc §2):**
  - **Confident-bias-lock (T1-code):** at any sensibly TUNED Q, a WORST-tier 2.5 m datum-biased cue makes the post-latch chi-square gate reject 81-100% of VALID camera corrections (0% at the broken Q=64 — the D1 Q-tune itself arms the failure). No honesty-audit layer sees it (correctness, not leakage).
  - **Bearing poisoning (T1-code):** Cartesian `correct_cue` leaks cross-range datum bias into lambda_hat (+6.3 deg @20 m -> +9.3 deg @10 m ~= 1.5 m cross-range aim error) despite concurrent 14 Hz camera updates — the exact door FusedTrack's polar split closes. Fix: polar-split the cue innovation; cue never touches the angle.
  - **Unbounded covariance survival (code-traced):** with `--fuse-midcourse` but not `--warm-handoff`, cue-shaped P survives the latch unbounded and the naive `correct_cue` counter reads 0 regardless. Fix: unconditional latch re-inflation (tracker-gated, unit-tested) + adaptive gate-recovery + `ekf_camera_gated_post_handoff` counter + gain-washout metric ("re-initialize or provably decay" is the testable standard, per ekf_design_brief).
  - **Metric blind spot (code-traced):** `tgt_n_hat/tgt_e_hat` always come from the alpha-beta TargetTracker — ADR-0037's position-RMSE null was structurally guaranteed. The capstone logs the EKF's own relative state and scores in the relative frame.
  - **Power + cost:** n=8 binomial metrics can't carry verdicts (McNemar needs 6/8 discordant); measured ~65-75 s/flight (5 batches) => verdict cells go to n=16 for minutes, not nights. Headline pre-named: L1-vs-L2 at EXPECTED tier on relative-frame track RMSE; WORST tier reframed as a SURVIVAL check (the mandate's actual intent), not the headline hunt.
- **Also this session (audit debt paid):** `scripts/audit_per_tick.py` extracts check_s2.sh's per-tick audit for batch sweeps (check_s2.sh untouched). Retro-audit of all 24 ADR-0038/0040 flights: **(a)/(b) clean 24/24** after two adjudicated calibrations — (i) a one-tick DASH->ENGAGE grace (the single (a) hit was the dash controller's final pre-latch steering carried into the first ENGAGE row, then held exactly); (ii) audit (c) gates only when POWERED (pre-CPA rows, n>=10, cmd-azimuth std>=2 deg): the deployment profile pins command azimuth to atan(v_perp/16)~4 deg (measured std 0.0-1.7 deg vs the ~20 deg S2 windows that calibrated the 0.7 bound). Three remaining powered-(c) fails are mechanically traced to SEEKER false-locks corrupting the lambda series itself (256788: 9/13 polluted ticks; run7: 15/25; 772247: 18.9-deg lambda jumps) — a detection-quality canary, not guidance dishonesty; the honest chain (static AST + structural cue-null + (a)/(b)) stands. Seeker v2's bar: powered-(c) failures disappear with the pollution.
- **Decision.** Design ratified as revised (`docs/fusion_capstone_design.md` v2). Build order P0-P4 queued AFTER seeker v2 closes (markerless baseline weights must be pinned first). The two offline-demonstrated failure modes (bias-lock, bearing poisoning) are pre-build fixes P1/P2, cheap to verify offline — not discoveries to be made in Gazebo after 100 flights.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Fable session, council of 3 Sonnet members; the numeric findings are the council's, executed against ekf_tracker.py + the mc_* logs.)
- **Addendum (same day — P0 executed offline; Q-tuning is MOOT; the real P0 is abort-scoring):** the two P0 screens ran ahead of schedule while seeker-v2 trained. (1) `scripts/ekf_q_replay.py` (16 flights, fixed-dt cadence, tail-excluded): NO over-wide-P signature at Q=64 — pooled NIS/gate metrics sit on the TIGHT side and every lower candidate is monotonically worse; recommendation confounded by cold-start/thin-data (56 pooled corrections) — treat as a screen only. (2) `scripts/ekf_lockout_forensics.py`: the D3.2 lockout-cascade hypothesis is DEAD on this data (max 2 consecutive rejections anywhere; 4/6 aborted flights had ZERO gate rejections) — but a perfect 6/6-vs-0/10 discriminator emerged: the ADR-0037 "aborts" are post-CPA bookkeeping (see the ADR-0037 addendum; misses 0.675–1.339 m, 3/6 beating their paired alpha-beta twin). **Design consequence:** D1's Q-tune prerequisite is RETIRED; P0 becomes (i) analyzer-level post-CPA abort reclassification (zero flight-code change, applies to ALL arms incl. a markerless-abort re-examination) and (ii) the L0 confirm arm re-gated on "EKF clean-rate parity under the corrected scoring". D3.2's adaptive gate-recovery stays in the design for its ORIGINAL purpose (the post-latch bias-lock, F1) — it was never reachable pre-latch on this data and that's now measured, not assumed. Design doc updated in place. *(i) BUILT same day (`scripts/abort_lens.py`; clean_corrected = raw OR post-CPA-abort with min_gt_range <= the ratified 2.5 m Pk radius): ekf arm 2/8 -> 8/8 corrected (rescued misses 0.675–1.339 m; r_hat at abort 52.8–106 m = the coasted-range branch evidence); alphabeta/apriltag 8/8 unchanged; markerless arms 6/8 UNCHANGED — their failures are real flybys (4.3–10.0 m) and ordinary breakoff-branch endings, not the LOST_TAG abort signature. Under corrected scoring the EKF is at full parity with alpha-beta on every metric.*

## ADR-0042 — Seeker v2 (hard negatives + range calibration): mechanism gates ALL PASS (pollution 0.751 -> 0.000, range 0.056 -> 0.935, audit-clean), outcome gates FAIL honestly (clean-rate 6/8 unchanged) — and the residual gap to the tag is BEARING-NOISE THROUGHPUT, not detection
- **Context.** ADR-0040 addendum named hard NEGATIVES the primary v2 lever, range calibration secondary. Gates PRE-REGISTERED before training (`docs/seeker_v2_plan.md`), including the declared NULL branch. Capture seeds (111/222/333) deliberately DISJOINT from the A/B evaluation seeds.
- **Method.** 3-flight capture pass (`capture_pass.sh` + passive recorder): 36 gt-projected flight positives + 694 hard negatives — the own PROP BLADES intrude at the frame TOP and the own GROUND SHADOW (drone-shaped) sits bottom-center; BOTH pass v1's L/R-edge-only self-mask; labels visually verified. Merge 531 pos / 354 neg (40% cap) -> identical v1 recipe (yolo11n @640, 80 ep, seed 0; dataset the ONLY variable) -> mAP50 0.954, mAP50-95 0.931 (v1: 0.733 — much tighter boxes). Calib sidecar span_eff=0.9216 m (IQR 0.908–0.933, n=88). Probe replicated on the v1 range list (2,3,4,5,6,7,9,12). Paired n=8 arm, master-seed 42, byte-identical plan (dry-run diffed). `scripts/check_seeker_v2.sh` exits 0 (6/6).
- **Pre-registered gate results.**
  - **G1 static probe: PASS** — 8/8 ranges at conf 0.95–0.99; calibrated ranges within ~3% of truth across 2–12 m.
  - **G2 PRIMARY clean-rate: FAIL** — 6/8, unchanged from v1. But 2a **pollution collapse: PASS, total** — near-boresight false-range fraction 0.751 -> **0.0000** (n=354 ticks, not one polluted detection). 2b half: seed 772247 FIXED (4.35 abort -> 1.57 clean); 33327 improved 5.95 -> 2.53 but aborted at 2.528 m — 28 mm over the 2.5 m rescue radius (abort_lens: post-CPA, honestly not rescued).
  - **G3 no-regression: FAIL** — 256788 regressed 2.50 clean -> 3.93 dirty; median gap-to-tag +0.658 -> +1.142 m; coverage 0.243 vs 0.248 (wash). 776647 stayed clean (2.54).
  - **G4 range: PASS** — median meas/gt 0.056 -> **0.935**.
  - **G5 honesty: PASS** — per-tick audit (the debt NEXT.md owed) green on ALL 8 flights INCLUDING powered check-(c), the pollution canary that 3 v1-family flights failed (ADR-0041); `cue_reads_post_handoff=0` 8/8; static suite 7/7.
- **The mechanism (final-second forensics on the 4 previously-tight seeds; `scripts/seeker/endgame_forensics.py`).** v2 detects the REAL target 78–89% of final-second ticks (v1: 25–35%, of which a chunk were false locks) — **detection persistence is NOT the binding constraint**. The binding constraint is bearing-noise throughput: the tag's endgame is near-ballistic (median |a_cmd|=0; vc rides the `VC_FLOOR_M_S=4.5` floor); v1's polluted estimates commanded IMPOSSIBLE corrections (median |a_cmd| up to 315 m/s² -> actuator-saturated -> flew ~straight -> **accidentally tight**); v2 commands moderate, ACHIEVABLE corrections (15–29 m/s²) off box-center bearings whose lambda-dot noise is ~1.5–2x the tag's subpixel corners — the vehicle FOLLOWS the noise, adding ~1 m endgame dispersion. **Two corrections to ADR-0040's reading fall out:** (1) v1's tight misses on its clean seeds were partly an artifact of its broken range channel (vc suppression + saturation), not superior tracking; (2) v1's "coverage doubled" was ~75% false detections — true-target coverage was ~0.06; v2's 0.24 is a genuine ~4x real-coverage gain.
- **Decision.** `drone_finetuned_v2.onnx` becomes the RECOMMENDED `MARKERLESS_NN_WEIGHTS` (honest instrument: zero pollution, calibrated range, audit-clean, kills the 10 m flyby class, fixes the v1 dropout aborts; failures are graceful near-boundary misses 2.5–3.9 m, not flybys). `--seeker markerless` default (off-the-shelf two-stage) unchanged. **Next levers (from the measured mechanism, NOT the pre-registered guess):** (a) terminal lambda-dot noise rolloff / box-center smoothing for markerless bearings — guidance-side, cheap, directly attacks the measured +1 m; (b) the fusion capstone (unchanged, ADR-0041). NOT more negatives (done), NOT range (done), NOT detection persistence (measured non-binding). The pre-registered G6 NULL branch is taken with this sharper mechanism substituted for its "detection persistence" guess.
- **Honesty.** Gates pre-registered before training; outcome gates logged FAILED; capture/eval seed disjointness; v1 artifacts byte-untouched (no sidecar); per-tick audit now standing for every arm. GPU flat (dxgk +19 benign over 9 boots).
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Fable main session + sonnet-workers; capture -> train -> probe -> paired A/B -> forensics, one day.)

## ADR-0043 — Terminal bearing-noise levers (gain rolloff + early freeze): pre-registered PRIMARY is NULL for both; the markerless +1.1 m gap-to-tag is bearing-QUALITY-limited at the kinematic ceiling
- **Context.** ADR-0042's measured mechanism (PN follows box-center bearing noise, p90 2.8 deg/tick vs the tag's subpixel ~0.00, with honest vc). Two flag-gated levers built (`--terminal-gain-scale`, `--terminal-freeze-range`, both default-off byte-identical), gates PRE-REGISTERED before flying (docs/seeker_v2_plan.md).
- **Arms (n=8 paired, master-seed 42, v2 weights):** B = gain 0.35 in ENGAGE; C = freeze at 6.0 m. `logs/mc_ab_tuned_v2_{gainB,freezeC}.csv`.
- **Result: PRIMARY NULL both.** Median gap-to-tag: v2 +1.142 -> B +1.339 (4/8 seeds improved, paired median -0.01 = wash) -> C +1.692 (1/8 improved, Pk@2.5 3/8 vs v2 4/8 — the pre-noted risk materialized: 6 m freeze forfeits real corrections while ZEM is nonzero). No sign-consistent shrink anywhere.
- **B's mechanism DID work** — endgame median |a_cmd| dropped 29.1->20.8 / 16.9->11.7 / 19.8->10.4 m/s2 with detection density intact (endgame_forensics) — the miss just doesn't respond: residual noise still passes, and the ~0.4 s terminal window is kinematically capped (ADR-0023). B also flew 8/8 clean (both v2 dirty seeds converted to ordinary endings, misses 3.93->3.46 / 2.53->2.18) — real stabilization but mostly abort-bookkeeping conversion; Pk@2.5 5/8 vs v2 4/8 = one flight at n=8, not adopted on a coin flip.
- **C additionally FAILS the standing per-tick audit 4/8 on check (c)** — a frozen terminal by definition stops tracking the camera, so early-freeze designs are structurally incompatible with the audit's cmd-follows-camera check; disqualifying for a default regardless of its (worse) miss numbers.
- **Decision.** NULL logged; both flags stay default-off and unadopted. The remaining +1.1 m median gap-to-tag on the deployment profile is accepted as the honest cost of box-center bearings vs subpixel tag corners at the kinematic ceiling. Paths that could still move it: measurement-side bearing quality (corner/keypoint refinement — real-hardware seeker territory, Stage-0+), or the fusion capstone's mid-course value (unaffected by this null; handoff-reach, not terminal miss, ADR-0041 D5). Markerless headline stands as ADR-0042 left it.
- **Honesty.** Pre-registered before flying; arm B audit 8/8 PASS; arm C audit failure disclosed above (mechanically explained, not a leak — commands HOLD rather than read anything). Both dirty-v2 seeds' "clean" conversions are noted as partly bookkeeping (misses still > 2.5 m Pk radius). GPU flat.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Two 8-flight arms, ~25 min sim; same-day design->pre-registration->fly->null.)

## ADR-0044 — Fusion capstone RESULT: mid-course cue fusion WORKS under the markerless seeker (8/8 paired win, both chronic failures rescued, survives WORST) — via the HAND-SET polar FusedTrack; covariance gating does not earn its keep and regresses under WORST bias
- **Context.** ADR-0041's ladder flown to completion (L0 n=8, L1/L2 n=16, L3 stress pair 2×8; ~56 flights, all master-seed-42 paired, v2 weights pinned, per-tick audits + abort lens on every arm). Headline amended to identically-logged metrics BEFORE L2 unblinded (docs/seeker_v2_plan.md; the pre-named relative-RMSE headline was computable only for the EKF arm — the F4-class logging asymmetry, disclosed).
- **L0 (EKF@64, fusion off, D1 gate): PASS-with-note.** clean raw 0/8 -> corrected 7/8 (vs alpha-beta's 6/8) — the ADR-0037 post-CPA bookkeeping reproduced and corrected; counters live (`ekf_cue_updates_post_handoff=0` everywhere); ONE flight (chronic 33327) failed audit (c) with 10 post-latch camera-gate rejections — the chi-square gate rejecting REAL near-CPA LOS dynamics faster than the N=3 recovery re-inflates (a known quirk carried forward, 2/16 recurrence in L1).
- **THE POSITIVE RESULT — L2 (alpha-beta + FusedTrack, fusion on) vs fusion-off, 8 shared seeds: tighter on 8/8 seeds (sign test p~=0.008), median paired delta −0.356 m, BOTH chronic dirty seeds rescued (256788: 3.93 DIRTY -> 2.28 clean; 33327: 2.53 DIRTY -> 2.22 clean), 16/16 raw clean, max 2.72 m.** And under the WORST-tier cue (datum 2.5 m + WORST sigma_R + dropout): **8/8 clean, max 2.88 m — full survival.** The ADR-0018 null legitimately flipped exactly as ADR-0034 hypothesized: with the markerless seeker's noisy camera (vs the clean tag), the ground cue carries real mid-course information again. First measured Gazebo win for `--fuse-midcourse` (previously "never-harmful, default-off").
- **The NEGATIVE result — covariance gating (L1, EKF + correct_cue) does not earn its keep.** EXPECTED tier vs L2: handoff-range wash (−0.30 m median), miss wash (8/16), but corrected clean **11/16 vs 16/16** (5 discordant pairs, p~=0.06 — suggestive) and max 4.93 vs 2.72. A VARIANCE signature, not a bias: L1 produced the project's tightest-ever markerless misses (0.151 m and 0.229 m — on the flights with the MOST post-latch gating, 63 and 31 rejections: deep terminal custody) alongside a fatter bad tail. **WORST tier: the survival check FAILS — corrected 5/8, max 7.66 m flyby** (vs hand-set's 8/8) — council finding F1 (tuned-gate bias-lock family) confirmed in flight. The polar-split DISCIPLINE ("the cue never touches the angle") is what matters; FIXED weights are sufficient AND more robust than live covariance weighting in this regime.
- **Decision.** (1) `--fuse-midcourse` (alpha-beta FusedTrack) is now RECOMMENDED for markerless configurations — the measured mid-course custody win; global default stays OFF (gated-path byte-stability; the apriltag path showed neutral, ADR-0018). (2) `--tracker ekf` + `correct_cue` stays a documented, fully-instrumented default-OFF option; alpha-beta remains the default tracker (now for measured reasons, not the withdrawn ADR-0037 artifact). (3) The capstone question is ANSWERED and the arc closes: jam-resistance thesis intact and now measurably supported end-to-end — ground cue carries mid-course custody, camera-only terminal finishes, and the boundary between them is instrumented and held (0 post-latch cue updates across all 32 EKF flights (96 redundant log lines, 3 layers each — count verified); audits green on L2/L3-arms; L1's 2/16 (c) fails are the disclosed near-CPA gating quirk, mechanically explained, not leakage).
- **Follow-ups (logged, not blocking):** near-CPA chi-square gating quirk (dof-2 threshold vs real terminal dynamics — revisit only if the EKF path is ever promoted); FusedTrack state logging + L2 track-RMSE re-fly if a track-level comparison is wanted; p_diag_at_latch to CSV; gain-washout metric; audit_per_tick fusion extension (cue-activity marker).
- **Honesty.** All arms pre-registered (incl. the mid-flight headline amendment committed before L2 landed); WORST framed as survival not headline (F6); corrected AND raw clean-rates reported everywhere; Wilson/sign-test language at n=8/16; the negative covariance-gating result reported with the same prominence as the positive fusion result. GPU flat across ~120 boots today.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08. (Design ADR-0041 -> P0 forensics -> P1/P2 build+review -> P3 ladder -> this result, one session.)

## ADR-0045 — Phase-2 plan adversarial review (pre-build): 18 confirmed findings folded in as binding amendments; 1 critical (sim-time leak in the real cue pipeline); WORST-tier jam tests were unrunnable as ratified
- **Context.** The builder ratified the Phase-2 sim-to-real plan (commits 4c9b70b/45c546d) and asked for a logic check before execution. A 5-dimension review (internal logic, factual grounding, F1 feasibility, honesty/reproducibility, gaps) ran with 2 adversarial skeptics per finding (47 subagents); 21 raw findings -> 18 confirmed, 3 refuted.
- **The critical one (sim-time leak, plan §5.6 #1).** A real two-process cue pipeline's NN+triangulation+UDP latency is WALL time; under RTF<1 it maps to a SMALLER sim-time delay, so the "real" pipeline delivers cues systematically fresher than reality — and fresher than the mock it's A/B'd against. Same failure class as ADR-0009. Fix folded in: t_sim stamped from the frame's sim-time header + a modeled sim-time latency floor + per-datagram latency logging + a latency-vs-ADR-0016-tier check in T19's pre-registered gate.
- **The one that would have hollowed out T20 (§5.6 #2).** In sim, rig and drone share one world frame — the real pipeline has ZERO natural datum bias, so the WORST-tier bias-lock tests and the "real spoof" promise were unrunnable as written. Fix: explicit `--datum-bias-m`/`--assumed-rig-pose` extrinsics perturbation + track-message spoof injector in ground_station.py.
- **Other majors folded in (plan §5.5 annotations + §5.6 #3-8):** F1 candidate (a) quietly reverts the flight cue to a recalibrated mock (T19 redefined if (a) wins); candidate (b)'s credible lever is RATE not resolution, and any resolution cut rescales the σ_R constant c=σ_d/(b·f_px) — T18 validates against the SCALED c; candidate (c) near-disqualified (ONE-sim rule + cross-instance clock/gt-bridge); m4_intercept.py owns the cue lifecycle so "consumes unchanged" is CueReader-only; ground-side honesty audit = subscription check (no gt/pose subs); T16 owes a rig-placement/FoV-wedge analysis vs the flown dash profile; the ground NN sees TWO drones (interceptor in dataset + identity gate from downlinked own-nav); T18 owes a σ_v-vs-rate analytic pre-step (informs T16's sensor rate). NEXT.md dependency/parallelism annotations corrected (T16 blocks T17; T17 ∥ design docs, sim-serialized with sim arms).
- **Refuted (recorded so they aren't re-litigated):** the plan does NOT internally contradict itself over §5.5-vs-A1 (council delegation is coherent); no repo evidence contradicts the llvmpipe-rendering suspicion (the probe's d3d12 arm tests it empirically); venv choice is immaterial at plan level.
- **Decision.** Plan amended in place (§5.5 annotations, new §5.6, cost-basis correction to ADR-0041); amendments are BINDING on the tasks they name. `scripts/probe_stereo_rtf.sh` built as the F1 evidence step (9-config RTF sweep incl. GPU/d3d12 arms). ADR numbering note: this entry takes 0045; the plan §6's indicative skeleton numbers shift by one (rig ADR becomes 0046 at write time, etc.).
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (evening, pre-build).

## ADR-0046 — Fork F1 DECIDED (council 3/3): the stereo rig ships as TRAJECTORY-MATCHED OFFLINE-RENDER REPLAY — real pipeline code runs LIVE at flight time on pre-rendered frames; live rendering is deferred behind a pre-registered probe gate; separate-sim-instance rejected
- **Context.** Phase-2 T16 needs the rig realization decided (one-way door). Evidence: RTF probe (scripts/probe_stereo_rtf.sh, logs/stereo_rtf_probe/results.csv, 10 configs), the F1 analytic pack (docs/rig_geometry_analysis.md), ADR-0045 amendments. Council: 3 independent members, same brief, full evidence pack.
- **The probe's decisive facts (single-run each, noise ±0.15 on loaded means; the two extremes unambiguous):** (1) UNSUBSCRIBED sensors are ~free — isolated RTF ≈1.0 even at 2×1920×1200; the cost is frame CONSUMPTION (transport/serialization), scaling with resolution×rate. (2) Full-res@30 Hz consumed = TOTAL collapse (0.038 mean — the demo-cam incident class); @10 Hz ≈0.54; every consumed-rig config dips to min 0.035–0.09 (OFFBOARD-entry risk band). (3) GALLIUM_DRIVER=d3d12 does NOT rescue the rig (0.57 at full-res@10) but makes the no-rig control rock-solid (0.9989 mean/0.9597 min vs 0.82/0.065 default) — a candidate global stability win, logged as its own follow-up. (4) The gz-topic-echo drain is transport-pessimistic but omits the real pipeline's NN inference load (member B) — live-(b)'s true cost is not bounded by this probe.
- **Votes.** A: (a), medium-high. B: (a) trajectory-matched replay flavor, high. C: (a)-primary with (b) gated behind a named probe arm, high. (c) rejected 3/3 (ONE-sim rule, cross-instance clock + gt-bridge honesty surface). No dissent on the pick; differences were flavor ((B) replay-real-code vs (A) statistics-replay) and how formally to gate (b)'s comeback (C).
- **Decision (Fable synthesis, adopting B's flavor + C's gate + A's T20 ruling).**
  1. **T16 builds:** `models/ground_stereo_rig/` (two cameras, 2.0 m baseline, HFOV 0.349 rad ≈ ADR-0017's 16 mm-equiv, FULL 1920×1200 — offline render pays no RTF tax) + `worlds/stereo_intercept.sdf` (markerless base + rig at `broadside_160m` per the analytic pack: 91%/91% coverage both dash directions, off-corridor; in-wedge fraction logged per flight per §5.6 #5) + a snapshot-capture harness extending the render_sim_dataset.py teleport pattern along the DETERMINISTIC mover path, sim-time-indexed, both directions.
  2. **Flight-time cue (T19 shape):** `ground_station.py` holds ZERO live camera subscriptions; it replays the pre-rendered frame sequence through the REAL detect+triangulate+track code LIVE during the flight, paced by sim time (the /clock-subscribing service-free mover pattern, ADR-0009 2nd addendum), emitting the unchanged :47800 JSON; `m4_intercept.py` gains `--cue-source {mock,stereo}` with mock as byte-identical default. Frames are target-exact (mover deterministic per seed), interceptor-approximate (guidance-reactive) — disclosed.
  3. **§5.6 #1:** t_sim stamped from capture-time sim clock; modeled latency floor sized from an offline wall-clock profile of the real pipeline; per-datagram latency logging. **#2:** `--datum-bias-m`/`--assumed-rig-pose` re-triangulates captured pairs under perturbed extrinsics (CPU-only, no re-render — cheaper than live per-bias flights) + emit-path track-message spoof injector.
  4. **(b) live rig: DEFERRED, not dead** — pre-registered comeback gate: probe arms low-res(≤960×540)+d3d12+rig AND a real binary-subscriber drain, n≥3–5 at idle; promote (b) only if loaded mean ≥0.8 AND min clears the ADR-0009 band through the OFFBOARD window. Optional `--cue-source stereo-live` demo mode may exist for portfolio footage only — never in a gate or batch.
  5. **T20 adoption ruling (A):** σ_R/σ_v measured by the real pipeline on real rendered frames COUNTS as "real stereo data" for T20's adoption gate; the "live spoof during live flight" demo is a SEPARATE claim, labeled separately.
  6. **Honesty language (all three):** README/ADR claims say "offline-rendered, trajectory-matched stereo frames processed by the real detection/triangulation/tracking pipeline at flight time over the real link" — never "live closed-loop stereo vision." The RTF bottleneck is a Gazebo-specific render/transport artifact with NO hardware analog (ADR-0016's split is two physically separate compute nodes) — the workaround must not be read as a design property.
- **Follow-ups logged:** d3d12 baseline validation (run an existing gate under GALLIUM_DRIVER=d3d12; if byte-equivalent behavior + stable RTF, adopt for all future runs — potential retirement of a whole class of RTF flakiness); σ_v-vs-jink shortfall (rig_geometry_analysis) carried into T20's assumptions regardless of F1.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (late evening). Council transcripts in session logs; brief archived in the session scratchpad.
- **Addendum (same night) — d3d12 follow-up EXECUTED: RECOMMENDED for new runs.** check_m3.sh (a real OFFBOARD intercept gate) PASSED under `GALLIUM_DRIVER=d3d12` — exit 0, final standoff error 0.027 m (< 0.5 m gate), detection coverage 1.000, env propagation verified in the gz process's /proc environ (not assumed). Combined with the probe rows (control loaded RTF 0.9989 mean/0.9597 min under d3d12 vs 0.82/0.065 default), d3d12 is the RECOMMENDED launch env for NEW Phase-2 runs. NOT hardwired into existing gate scripts (gated paths keep their env); adoption bar for batches: the first d3d12 batch arm runs a paired-seed sanity comparison vs a stock-renderer arm before d3d12 batches feed any A/B conclusion (statistics-before-verdicts; one gate pass ≠ batch parity). Same run captured the full trajectory-matched sweep: 56 L/R pairs (28/direction, exact match to the harness math), `logs/rig_captures/full_sweep_20260709T015530Z` — T17's training input + T18's replay sequence.

## ADR-0047 — T17 discrimination strategy (BUILDER ruling): acquire-then-track — single-class drone detector for initial lock, track custody thereafter; NO drone-type classification
- **Context.** ADR-0045 amendment §5.6 #6 required the interceptor airframe in the ground dataset (as a distinguishable class or hard negative) so the rig never hands the seeker the wrong drone. The builder rejected the classification half of that (2026-07-08, late evening): in the field both airframes are FPV-class quads — often near-identical — so a type discriminator is brittle, and a sim-trained one would learn Gazebo-specific appearance cues that don't transfer (the exact hard-won T24/nn_transfer_plan lesson).
- **Decision (builder's, adopted).** Split acquisition from maintenance: (1) **Acquisition** — the ground NN only ever answers "is there a drone." Under launch-on-detect the interceptor is still on the ground at lock time, so exactly one airborne object exists and no discrimination is needed. Single-class detector; the interceptor airframe is NOT added to the training set (reverses the §5.6 #6 "distinguishable class or hard negative" half). (2) **Maintenance** — detect-then-track: once locked, custody is held by frame-to-frame track continuity, not a fresh per-frame classification; a second drone entering the frame is simply not the held track.
