The expensive detector initiates; a lightweight tracker sustains (cheaper, and the standing detection-floor logic in stereo_design.md already assumes detect-then-track). (3) **Identity gate** — the interceptor DOWNLINKS its own nav position (legitimate ground-side info, ADR-0016; not a honesty-boundary breach — it is the interceptor telling the ground where it is, not the ground reading gt); the tracker ignores any detection coincident with that position and, on re-acquisition after a track drop, re-locks the detection that is NOT at the interceptor's location. So type classification is never needed, even on recovery.
- **What changes.** T17 keeps needing a real single-class "drone" detector but DROPS the two-class dataset / interceptor-in-training-set requirement — less to render, less to label, no reliance on a type distinction that would not survive real FPV-vs-FPV contact. Effort shifts from "curate a discriminating dataset" to "get the detect→track handoff and the drop/re-acquire logic right" — where the real failure modes live (track drift, identity swap when the two drones genuinely overlap in the 2D image).
- **Honest weak point (log in the T23 audit).** When the interceptor and target are genuinely 2D-coincident in the rig's view, appearance cannot separate them — only their 3D/range separation (the rig triangulates both) or the downlinked interceptor position disambiguates. This is a real limit, not designed away.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (late evening). Amends ADR-0045 §5.6 #6; binds T17/T18.

## ADR-0048 — Fork F2 DECIDED (council 3/3): reuse the :47800 UDP JSON cue wire; m4_intercept.py spawns ground_station.py exactly as it spawns the mock (--cue-source {mock,stereo}, default mock); sim-time discipline via a DUAL-GATE buffer-release
- **Context.** T19 (compute split + real link) needed its wire format, process lifecycle, and reproducibility guarantee decided before build. Council: 3 independent members, brief in session scratchpad, full code + ADR-0045/0046/0016 evidence. UNANIMOUS on both headline picks; each re-derived them independently rather than deferring to the plan's recommendation.
- **1. Wire format — REUSE :47800 UDP JSON `{seq,t_sim,x,y,z(,vx,vy,vz)}`.** triangulate.py already emits this exact schema (committed); CueReader already parses optional velocity via msg.get(); a binary format buys only optical resemblance to ADR-0016's ~90-byte real message, which is a HARDWARE bandwidth argument (7.2 kbps vs a 32 kbps SiK radio) the sim never enforces on loopback — and would fork CueReader (two parse paths through every gated --handoff path) for zero functional gain. The ~90-byte resemblance is captured as a WRITTEN byte-size derivation in the T19 ADR, not code. A binary `--cue-wire` variant, if ever wanted, is an opt-in non-gating demo (same treatment as ADR-0046's stereo-live).
- **2. Lifecycle — m4_intercept.py spawns ground_station.py**, gated `--cue-source {mock,stereo}`, default mock. Categorical argument (member B): the codebase draws a firm line between SHARED infra (PX4/Gazebo, launched once by the gate) and per-flight SCENARIO ACTORS (mover, cue mock, spawned BY m4); ground_station.py replays this run's seed/geometry → scenario actor → same side as the mover/mock. This also gets the UDP bind-before-send race right for free (CueReader binds the socket in __init__ before the Popen), keeps batch-seed forwarding single-owner (mc_batch → m4 → the producer), and reuses vetted terminate/kill logic. Gate cleanup adds `ground_station.py` beside the existing `pkill -f s2_cue_mock.py` in check_s2.sh (in the SCRIPT file — inline-pkill self-kill rule still applies).
- **3. Sim-time discipline (§5.6 #1) — the council's key technical finding (member B, tasked to attack it).** Port s2_cue_mock's pending-queue buffer-and-release verbatim: t_sim stamped from each pre-rendered frame's BAKED capture-time sim clock (immune to this flight's live RTF); `deliver_t_sim = t_sim_sample + latency_floor` where latency_floor is a CONSTANT from an offline wall-clock profile. **But buffer-release-on-sim-time only neutralizes wall jitter IF the compute result is actually READY when sim-time crosses the deadline — under single-GPU contention (Gazebo render + the T17 ONNX detector on the one 4070) the floor can be violated in the "not ready yet" direction, and blindly releasing then ships a stale/incomplete result: a CORRECTNESS bug, not hidden jitter.** MANDATED fix: the release condition is a CONJUNCTION — `sim_time >= deliver_t AND result_ready`; any tick where result_ready is the binding clause is logged with a distinct `floor_violated` marker; the offline latency-floor profile is measured WITH Gazebo concurrently rendering at flight resolution/rate (an isolated profile is optimistic exactly where it matters); the T19 pre-registered gate asserts delivered sim-time latency never reads BELOW the floor across a batch. On the receive side nothing changes — CueReader already computes staleness sim-time-vs-sim-time, so loopback wire jitter was already quarantined from guidance timing across all existing gated flights.
- **4. Reproducibility guarantee — scope + test (member C's AST-pin + member B's re-run).** (a) s2_cue_mock.py bytes UNCHANGED (SHA-256 pin — its RNG stream/schedule can't change if the file can't). (b) CueReader class body zero-diff (ast/hash pin — per §5.6 #3 the one thing that must never change). (c) mock-branch spawn argv pinned at the AST-TOKEN level, NOT a raw string match (wrapping today's lines in `if cue_source=="mock":` changes leading whitespace — a string test would false-fail; use the ast idiom from tests/test_honesty_static.py, not the exact-multiline-string idiom from test_ekf_tracker.py). (d) default `args.cue_source=="mock"` asserted. (e) `grep -L -- --cue-source` over every gated check_*.sh must list them ALL (no gated script edited to accommodate the flag). (f) **the mock and stereo branches must NOT share a "convenience" helper** — keep the mock block textually untouched-and-moved under a conditional (the ADR-0014 addendum CSV-column pollution precedent). (g) one-time paired-seed gate re-run: the mock's own logs/s2_cue_*.csv byte-identical before/after; flight CSVs clear EXISTING thresholds within the documented ~1 m variance. NOT bit-identical flight CSV — ADR-0013 physics nondeterminism makes that a false-failure trap (statistics-before-verdicts).
- **5. Interpreter (all three) — do NOT copy the mock's `sys.executable` spawn.** ground_station.py needs .venv-seeker (onnxruntime for the T17 detector), not the main venv; use an explicit `GROUND_STATION_VENV_PYTHON` path constant (mirroring how CUE_MOCK_SCRIPT is a computed path), failing loudly if missing. A naive copy crashes a default-venv `--cue-source stereo` flight with a child ImportError.
- **Risks carried into T19.** (i) Compute-bound floor violation (mitigated by the dual-gate + contention-profiled floor). (ii) NN cold-start (onnxruntime model load) vs `CUE_WAIT_TIMEOUT_S=15 s` — that timeout is WALL/monotonic, so RTF-insensitive but a real wall budget; if measured slow, move the spawn point earlier in boot, keep m4 as owner (do NOT switch to external orchestration). (iii) NN weight-load inflating the ~65-75 s/flight basis at Monte-Carlo scale → only then reconsider a batch-reused long-lived process (default/mock path unchanged regardless).
- **Confidence.** All three: HIGH on wire+lifecycle (ratified ADR-0046 text + committed matching code + independent structural arguments), MEDIUM on the sim-time mechanization (pre-build design; ground_station.py + check_t19.sh don't exist yet). Flip evidence (B): an offline profile under concurrent Gazebo render showing compute routinely exceeds the floor even at RTF<1 → the dual-gate would bind routinely and a fixed-floor design should give way to an explicit compute-bound latency model.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (late evening, pre-build). Council transcripts in session logs; brief in scratchpad. Binds T19.

## ADR-0049 — T17 ground detector SHIPPED + gate PASS (verifier-confirmed TRUE) — but NECESSARY-NOT-SUFFICIENT: proves in-domain single-operating-point lock, NOT acquisition-envelope robustness
- **What was built.** ground_v1 single-class "drone" detector (ADR-0047 acquire-then-track): yolo11n fine-tuned on 108 positives (projected from T16's 56-pose sweep, gt→label offline, honesty-clean) + 60 negatives; scripts/{build_ground_dataset,label_rig_captures,eval_ground_detector,capture_rig_negatives}.py + check_t17.sh. Trained to epoch 100 (resumed once mid-run on CPU — .venv-seeker-train is CPU-only torch, ~44 s/epoch, [[venv-seeker-train-cpu-only-torch]]); ground_v1.pt byte-identical to final best.pt (md5 f4c0aff…).
- **Gate PASS is TRUE (verifier re-ran, exit 0).** near/far recall+precision = 1.0, neg-clean = 1.0. Verifier confirmed NO cheating: real IoU-matched TP/FP/FN (degenerate predict-nothing/predict-everything both fail), held-out split BY POSE SEQUENCE (0 sequences straddle train/val), pixel-only inference (gt only builds labels offline). data.yaml single-class (no interceptor, per ADR-0047).
- **But the PASS is NECESSARY-NOT-SUFFICIENT — quantitatively severe (verifier, data-derived):** (1) val positives span **160.06–161.93 m — a 1.87 m window**; "near/far" is an OFF-BORESIGHT-ANGLE median split (1.5°–10.1°), NOT a range sweep. (2) EVERY val box is **exactly 35.2×35.2 px** — one fixed apparent target size; the only size-varying boxes (14.4, 7.6 px) landed in TRAIN, so the ~10–12 px detection FLOOR is **untested**. (3) all **60 negatives are byte-identical** (one 3-RGB-value blank-sky frame — worse than the README's "1 per camera"; the flat textureless world can't provide clutter diversity even though capture_rig_negatives.py swept 3 positions for it). (4) train/val are **near-pixel-identical** (~0.3° apart, ~0.02% of pixels differ) — "held out" = interpolation from immediate neighbors, not a distinct viewpoint.
- **Root cause (a real finding about the T16 capture geometry).** The single broadside_160m rig pose + a target that only crosses the wedge (cross-range dash) yields captures at ONE range (~160 m) and one apparent size. To test the acquisition envelope you need MULTI-RANGE captures (target approaching the rig, or multiple rig standoffs) and background/lighting diversity the current flat world lacks.
- **Decision.** ground_v1 PASSES its (honest but narrow) pre-registered thresholds and is ADOPTED as the **pipeline driver** for T18/T19 — it demonstrably locks the drone at handoff range in-domain, which is all T18's triangulation validation needs to proceed. It is EXPLICITLY NOT the deployment detector and earns NO "works" claim beyond the single operating point. Any envelope/generalization/lethal framing requires a T17-v2 (multi-range + diverse negatives, ideally Stage-0 real data per [[nn_transfer_plan]]). Weights/dataset stay gitignored (regenerable artifact, matches the v2 precedent); the four scripts + gate are committed.
- **Binds T18's interpretation:** a detector overfit to near-duplicate frames will produce optimistically small centroid error, so T18's NN-mode σ_R / implied-σ_d at 160 m is a BEST-CASE lower bound, not a robust envelope measurement; and σ_R∝R² scaling is untestable on single-range data. Logged for the T23 audit as a named sim-to-real gap.
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (late evening). Verifier transcript in session logs.

## ADR-0050 — T18 PIVOTAL CHECKPOINT (real-detector σ_R vs the physics model): NO RED FLAG at handoff range, but best-case/thin — real triangulation on real detections comes in AT/UNDER the mock's assumption, so the fusion conclusions are not threatened; NOT yet conclusive (single range, n=10, train-adjacent)
- **The question (plan §A3, "may revise every fusion conclusion").** Does the REAL detect→triangulate pipeline produce range noise σ_R consistent with the c=4.45e-05 the mock/fusion arc assumed? Method: run ground_v1 on T16's 56-pose capture, take box-center as the stereo centroid, triangulate via the committed ground_station.triangulate (TRUE rig pose), compare range error vs gt. New harness scripts/t18_nn_sigma_validation.py (reuses the perfect-centroid harness's math unchanged; .venv-seeker-train); full notes docs/t18_nn_validation_notes.md.
- **Result — no red flag, with the honest slice.** Detection at handoff: every LABELED frame detected (miss rate 4/112 = 3.6%, all 4 are label-rejected off-frame/too-small, not detector misses). σ_R on the CLEAN slice (pose-pairs where NEITHER camera frame was in ground_v1's train split): **0.48 m at ~161 m**, implied σ_d ≈ 0.615 px — UNDER both the resolution-scaled matching-only model (0.95 m) and the mock's full-budget EXPECTED (1.15 m). So real triangulation at handoff range is at least as good as assumed → the ADR-0044 fusion conclusions do not need revising downward.
- **The naive number is a TRAP, correctly quarantined.** Running the detector over ALL 112 frames gives σ_R = 8.00 m (~7–8× the models) — but that is a LEAKAGE + OUTLIER artifact, not a real σ_R: (1) 88/108 (81%) of labeled frames were in ground_v1's OWN train split (near-memorized), and (2) two poses (seq 2, 30) at the small-disparity dash entry produced +40 m residuals because a low-confidence-side box center shifted a couple px, and at ~161 m true disparity is only ~5 px so Z=b·f_px/disparity is ill-conditioned. The worker surfaced the leakage itself (not in the brief), refused to quote 8.00 m as a real estimate, and built the CLEAN subset as the honest signal — the plan's "too-good-OR-too-bad number is a bug to investigate" discipline, applied.
- **Why it's NOT conclusive (binding caveats, docs/t18_nn_validation_notes.md).** (1) Single range band ~160–163 m — σ_R∝R² UNTESTED. (2) implied σ_d uses the same pinhole projection triangulate() inverts — a matching-noise proxy, not an independent pixel-accuracy GT. (3) even the CLEAN split is train-ADJACENT (~0.3° apart, near-pixel-identical, per ADR-0049) → a BEST-CASE lower bound, not out-of-domain generalization. (4) n=10 is thin and the two internal noise statistics cross-check only ~0.33× (wide sampling variance). Read CLEAN as "how good the pipeline looks under near-ideal conditions," not proof.
- **Decision.** (1) T18's triangulation+track pipeline is VALIDATED as arithmetically sound and its real-detection σ_R at handoff is IN-FAMILY with (best-case: better than) the mock — the pivotal checkpoint raised no alarm, so T19 is UNBLOCKED to build on ground_v1 as the cue driver. (2) The σ_R question is NOT closed: fusion ADOPTION (T20) stays gated on a genuinely independent capture (builder's "adopt only on real stereo data" sequencing holds — the current data is best-case). (3) **Consolidated follow-up (serves three needs at once): a second, independent multi-range capture — different rig pose(s)/standoff so the target spans a RANGE of distances, different lighting, never touching ground_v1's training — validates σ_R∝R² (T18), tests detector generalization (the T17-v2 the ADR-0049 gap needs), and provides the truly held-out σ_R for T20 adoption. One campaign, three deliverables.**
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (late evening). Harness + notes committed; per-frame CSVs in logs/t18_nn_sigma_validation/ (gitignored).

## ADR-0051 — Fork F4 (T19b): in sim, PRECOMPUTE detection on the deterministic frames and replay cached centroids through LIVE triangulation — do NOT run detection live during the flight (GPU-live is a hardware concern, and an optional future sim enhancement). ⚑ FLAG: refines ADR-0046's "live detection" language on new evidence — builder may veto.
- **New evidence (T19a, measured).** ground_station replaying real ground_v1 detection LIVE costs ~0.85–1.25 s/frame-pair on CPU (.venv-seeker onnxruntime is a CPU-only build — providers = [CPU, Azure], no CUDA). At a 10 Hz cue that is ~10× too slow; the dual-gate correctly HELD every frame (n_floor_violations = 6/6) rather than shipping stale data — proving the gate load-bearing, but also proving live CPU detection makes the delivered cue paced by wall-clock compute under RTF, i.e. the exact ADR-0045 #1 sim-time leak (in the too-SLOW direction). GPU would need an onnxruntime-gpu + CUDA/cuDNN install (WSL version-matching risk); it is NOT installed.
- **The key realization.** The rig frames are PRE-RENDERED and DETERMINISTIC (ADR-0046), and detection on a fixed frame is deterministic → running the detector live vs precomputed on the SAME frames yields BYTE-IDENTICAL cue values. Live detection's only added effect in sim is an uncontrolled compute latency — which ADR-0048 §3 explicitly wanted modeled as a CONSTANT floor, not inherited as live jitter. So precompute is output-identical AND sim-time-cleaner.
- **Options.** (a) onnxruntime-GPU live (~10–30 ms, most literal to ADR-0046, but an install + CUDA-matching risk, and still adds live jitter the design didn't want); (b) **precompute detection once over the capture set → cache centroids → station.py replays cached centroids through LIVE triangulation + the modeled latency floor** (identical output, zero flight-time detection cost, sim-time-clean, no install); (c) big floor + reduced rate (degrades the cue).
- **Decision (educated, reversible → logged not councilled): (b).** In a deterministic sim, precompute is strictly better — same cue, controlled timing, no GPU/CUDA dependency. The REAL detect code still runs on the REAL rendered frames (just ahead of the flight), so ADR-0046's "real pipeline on real frames" intent holds; triangulation + the track filter + the :47800 emit + the dual-gate all still run LIVE. GPU-live detection is the correct model for HARDWARE (where detection genuinely runs concurrently on the Pi/Jetson) and remains an optional future sim enhancement if concurrent-detection realism is ever wanted — station.py already contains the live path, so this is one flag, fully reversible.
- **Honesty (T23 audit note).** README/ADR language: "the real ground detector is run on the real rendered rig frames; triangulation, tracking, the datum/spoof injection, and cue delivery run live over the real link, with pipeline latency modeled by a measured floor." The one sim-artifact to disclose: detection is precomputed rather than concurrent — output-identical here because the frames are deterministic, but it means the sim does NOT exercise detection/flight GPU CONTENTION (a real hardware effect) — carry that into the T23 gap table alongside the already-noted zero-stereo-sync-error and single-range-capture gaps.
- **⚑ BUILDER FLAG.** This adjusts the "runs the real detect code LIVE during the flight" wording the builder ratified in ADR-0046. The RESULT is identical (deterministic frames) and it's fully reversible (station.py keeps the live path behind a flag), but flagging explicitly per the honesty boundary: if the builder wants literal concurrent live detection for the demo/story, the path is onnxruntime-GPU (option a) — say so and it's a small change.
- **Reshapes T19b:** part-1 = a detection-cache builder (offline, run ground_v1 over a capture set → centroids.csv) + station.py `--centroid-cache` replay mode (offline-testable, low risk); part-2 = m4 `--cue-source` wiring + check_t19.sh flight gate (the guidance-core integration, needs a flight).
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (late evening). Evidence: ADR-0050/T19a; station.py smoke run.

## ADR-0052 — T19 live flight found a real CLOCK-EPOCH bug: the pre-baked cache clock has no relationship to the live flight clock, so the cue bursts and the interceptor sees only a stale frame. T19 NOT validated. Fix = epoch-rebase + mover-trigger co-start (+ a real sim-to-real time-sync insight).
- **What happened.** The m4 --cue-source stereo wiring (ace11d0) landed with all repro pins green (existing paths byte-identical) and the live-emit path smoke-passed. But the first real INTERCEPT flight (check_t19.sh, 535616b) FAILS its key assertion: the delivered cue does not track the target — cue-vs-gt error 24.36 m, byte-identical across 2 flights (deterministic, not noise). Assertions 1/2/3/5/6 pass (stereo branch spawns, cues received, handoff, audit-clean, completes); assertion 4 (alignment) fails.
- **Root cause (flight-diagnosed, precise).** station.py's cache frame_t_sim is baked 0–2.7 s from the CAPTURE run's own clock. In the flight, station.py spawns at CUE_WAIT entry — AFTER boot+settle+arm+takeoff+OFFBOARD — when the live /clock is already ~23 s. The dual-gate `release when sim_t >= frame_t_sim + latency` therefore sees EVERY frame as long-overdue and releases all 26 in a single microsecond burst. CueReader is latest-wins (no queue), and the interceptor's 20 Hz poll only ever catches the LAST datagram (cache seq 27 = the target's dash-END, y=5.0) while the real target is still parked at the dash-START (y=29.3) — hence 24.36 m. NOT a trajectory-matching error (the mover trajectory was correctly matched to the cache: r2l, y0=29.3, v=−9); a clock-EPOCH error.
- **Why the smoke test missed it (methodology lesson).** The 2026-07-08 live-emit smoke test ran station.py immediately after boot, at sim-clock≈0, so the cache's 0–2.7 s baked times aligned with the live clock BY ACCIDENT and streamed correctly. Only a real flight (station.py spawned ~23 s in) exposed the epoch gap. Reinforces "Lab ranks, Gazebo decides" and "a too-clean intermediate result is a bug to investigate": the smoke green was real but not sufficient.
- **★ This bug IS a sim-to-real finding (T23).** The cache-clock-vs-flight-clock epoch mismatch is the SIM analog of the exact hardware problem ADR-0015/0017 flagged: the ground rig and the drone are separate clocks, and without a shared time datum (PPS/RTK time-sync) their track timestamps don't align. The sim just DEMONSTRATED, end-to-end, why ground↔drone time synchronization is load-bearing for a cued handoff — a headline T23 gap-audit entry, not just a code bug.
- **Fix (design work, T19b-continued — NOT a one-liner).** Two coupled pieces: (1) **epoch rebase** — station.py, on entering live streaming, records t0 = current /clock and delivers each frame at `t0 + frame_t_sim + latency` (stream the cache over [t0, t0+2.7 s], not against absolute baked times); OPTIONALLY a bounded CueReader queue so a burst can't collapse to one stale sample. (2) **mover-trigger co-start** — the deeper issue: S2 spawns the mover at the interceptor-triggered DASH (target parked during CUE_WAIT), but the cache assumes the target is ALREADY moving from its t=0. So epoch-rebasing to station.py's spawn is still wrong unless the mover's motion timeline is co-aligned. Options: (a) co-spawn the mover with station.py at a shared trigger and rebase both to it; (b) bake a parked→moving profile into the cache matching the flight's CUE_WAIT→DASH; (c) rethink whether the pre-baked-cache approach fits the interceptor-triggered-mover architecture at all (may reopen a slice of F1/ADR-0046). This is a genuine design decision, plausibly council-worthy.
- **Status.** T19 wiring DONE + proven-safe (pins); live emit path WORKS; live INTERCEPT blocked on this epoch/trigger alignment. The gate (check_t19.sh) correctly detects it and stays as the close-out check. T20/T21/T25 that need the real cue in a flight are blocked behind this; T20 fusion adoption was already gated on the independent capture (ADR-0050).
- **Date:** 2026-07-08 (late evening). Flight CSVs logs/m4_intercept_pronav_20260709T0503*/0507*Z.csv; the flight worker's diagnosis in session logs.
- **RESOLVED (2026-07-09 early, commit e22d6a8) — T19 VALIDATED + verifier-gated.** Fix = the designed two parts + one principled addition, all gated so the mock/default path is byte-identical (5 repro pins green): (1) **epoch-rebase** — station.py in LIVE mode records t0=/clock at stream start and delivers each frame at t0+frame_t_sim+latency (streams over [t0,t0+2.7s], no burst); (2) **mover co-start** — for --cue-source stereo ONLY, m4 spawns the target mover at CUE_WAIT entry (co-epoch with station.py) instead of DASH, so the target's motion aligns with the cache (also MORE realistic — a hostile target is inbound before launch); (3) **--min-conf gate (0.5)** — station.py rejects a detection below threshold via the EXISTING miss path (a real rig rejects low-conf/edge/small-disparity hits), dropping the one ADR-0050 seq=2 outlier (conf_r=0.32 → ~43 m triangulation) that alone tripped the MAX bar. Progression: 24.36 m constant → (epoch) 1.0 m median but 43 m max → (conf-gate) **0.99 m median / 2.06 m max, check_t19.sh exit 0**. Independent verifier re-flew it (its own run: median 1.03 / max 2.03), recomputed cue-vs-gt from raw CSV (matched), confirmed NO gt/pose leak (station.py subscribes /clock only; gt→audit-log only, never into triangulate which uses assumed_pose), the cue tracks the MOVING target tick-by-tick (gt_y 25.66→12.60 over 1.4 s), post-handoff guidance traces to CAMERA measurements with 0/73 cue leakage, conf-gate drops exactly 1 frame via the ordinary miss path (fidelity, not cheat), mover co-start strictly stereo-gated, all 5 pins + full suite green. **T19 is the drone flying on a genuinely computed stereo cue — the whole T16→T17→T18→T19 real-pipeline spine now works end-to-end.** Disclosed-not-hidden residuals: single-flight n=1 (~1 m median near the terminal-dropout noise floor), detection precomputed not concurrent (ADR-0051), latency floor not yet Gazebo-concurrent-profiled (T19b OWES).

## ADR-0053 — Multi-range capture (ADR-0050 consolidated follow-up): σ_R∝R² VALIDATED across 50-160 m at the physics/pipeline level (exponent 2.003, c matches model 0.98x); but the real detector does NOT generalize across range (systematic bias out-of-domain) — T17-v2 multi-range training now justified by EVIDENCE, and is T20's prerequisite
- **Context.** ADR-0050 flagged two coupled gaps: σ_R∝R² was UNTESTED (all captures ~160 m) and ground_v1 is validated at one operating point (ADR-0049 necessary-not-sufficient). This is the one-campaign-three-needs follow-up. Rig placed at 4 standoffs {50,90,130,160 m} via live `gz set_pose` on the static ground_stereo_rig model (mechanism verified empirically: a 35 px dot at 160 m vs a rotor-resolved 100+ px object at 50 m — the camera extrinsics genuinely moved, not a stale render); target teleported to a wedge-verified y-grid at each; 20/20 poses captured, one sim boot, d3d12. New harnesses scripts/multirange_{capture,sigma_fit,nn_detection}.py + run_multirange_capture.sh.
- **PIVOTAL RESULT — σ_R∝R² HOLDS (perfect-centroid mode, t18_sigma_validation reused).** Measured σ_R vs model at EXPECTED tier: 50 m 0.092 (-0.3%), 90 m 0.298 (-0.01%), 130 m 0.622 (+0.06%), 160 m 0.942 (+0.09%). Power-law fit: **σ_R = 3.62e-05·R^2.003, log-log R²=1.0000** — exponent 2.003 vs physics 2.0 (consistent); fitted c=3.62e-05 vs resolution-scaled EXPECTED 3.67e-05 = 0.98x (essentially exact), vs the mock's full-budget 4.45e-05 = 0.81x (expected — mock adds calibration/sync/distortion the matching-only harness omits). **This CLOSES T18's "single-range, untested scaling" gap at the geometry/pipeline level:** Gazebo's rendered camera geometry matches the assumed pinhole across a 3.2x range span, and triangulate.py correctly generalizes the R² relation across 4 independently-rendered poses.
- **HONEST SCOPE.** Perfect-centroid mode validates the GEOMETRY + triangulation CODE, NOT a real detector's noise scaling. That is the separate, harder question the bonus pass probes.
- **BONUS — the real detector does NOT generalize across range (the more consequential finding).** ground_v1 (trained only ~160 m, ADR-0049) gets 100% RECALL at all 4 standoffs, but recall ≠ accuracy: at 50 m its box is undersized + mis-centered → **-6.3 m systematic bias (12.5% of range)** with deceptively tight σ=0.12 m (the bias is consistent, not random); at 90/130/160 m bias <~1.5 m, σ 0.5-0.8 m (n=5/standoff — thin, a trend not a statistic). **The mechanism: the box-size regression head is NON-MONOTONIC with range out-of-domain** (50 m boxes 64 px diag < 90 m boxes 81 px diag, despite being closer/larger in reality) — a genuine out-of-domain generalization failure.
- **Decision.** (1) σ_R∝R² is VALIDATED at the physics/pipeline level — the fusion arc's R²-scaling assumption is sound across the envelope, not just at handoff. (2) The real-detector out-of-domain bias is now MEASURED, not assumed → **T17-v2 (multi-range training) is justified by hard evidence and is the PREREQUISITE for T20's held-out σ_R and any across-envelope claim** — do NOT adopt a fusion default on ground_v1 multi-range data (it's biased out-of-domain). (3) The multi-range capture campaign + harnesses exist now (reusable for T17-v2's dataset). (4) Same mechanism-vs-outcome pattern as ADR-0042/0050: geometry/code sound, the LEARNED component is the limitation — measured honestly.
- **Date:** 2026-07-09 (early; continues the 2026-07-08 Phase-2 session). Logs/multirange_*/ (gitignored); scripts committed.

## ADR-0054 — T20 fusion-refinement DESIGN ratified (judge-panel of 3 independent approaches): refine the WINNER (hand-set polar FusedTrack, 8/8 WORST), NOT the regressed EKF — a range-agreement monitor (scalar along-LOS de-bias vs a shadow camera-only track, camera-lock-streak-gated) + camera-favoring confidence. Design only; execution + a possible NULL gated on the mock prototype and real T17-v2 data.
- **Context.** ADR-0044 left the open question: the hand-set polar FusedTrack fuses the mid-course cue and survives WORST 8/8, but the covariance-gated EKF fusion FAILED (bias-lock: it RSS's the persistent datum bias into measurement noise R, the gate tightens around the spoofed offset, then post-latch rejects the true camera — 5/8, 7.66 m flyby). Plan Track B/T20 mandate: (i) bias-state augmentation (estimate the datum offset, don't average it), (ii) camera-favoring confidence, keep polar discipline. Designed via a judge-panel workflow (3 independent architectures → adversarial synthesis; the ADR-0041 "design-by-executed-experiment" spirit).
- **The 3 approaches + ranking.** (1) Bias-state EKF — 5th along-LOS bias state, estimated from cue-minus-camera disagreement, subtracted, discarded at latch (elegant, well-grounded — but scored 6/10 because it REOPENS the chi-square camera gate that produced the ADR-0044 failure, and inherits 2-D weak-observability). (2) Camera-favoring confidence schedule — lock-streak decay × observed-disagreement (6.5/10, lowest-complexity). (3) **Range-Agreement Monitor + scalar de-bias + camera-favoring confidence — 8/10, WINNER.**
- **The key insight (why the winner wins).** Build on the PROVEN substrate — the alpha-beta hand-set FusedTrack (ADR-0044 WORST 8/8 champ), which has NO camera gate — so "the gate rejects the true camera" is UNREACHABLE BY CONSTRUCTION. Then graft Design 1's bias mechanism as a 1-D SCALAR (not a 5th EKF state) + Design 2's lock-streak gate. Gets bias-state's whole value (bias observable from the camera anchor, subtracted, discarded at latch) WITHOUT reopening the covariance gate, WITHOUT the 2-D weak-observability, and preserves polar discipline for free (a scalar along-LOS quantity is orthogonal to bearing).
- **The design (adds ~20 scalar lines to FusedTrack; NO new EKF, NO camera-side change; flag `--fuse-agreement-monitor`, default OFF, byte-identical when off per ADR-0048 pin discipline).** (1) SHADOW camera-only range track rng_cam (alpha-beta, full-gain, updated ONLY in update_camera) = the independent bias-free along-LOS reference — disagreement measured against IT, not the fused r_hat (so camera drift can't masquerade as cue disagreement). (2) CAMERA-LOCK-STREAK GATE: freeze the bias estimator + de-bias unless cam_streak ≥ S_lock (~8) AND the camera is fresh — the single most important robustness graft (b/position separation is observable only when both sources are live). (3) ROBUST SCALAR BIAS b: d_k = cue_range − rng_cam.x_hat; b = (1−λ_b)·b + λ_b·Huber-clip(d_k), + a soft OU leak toward 0 (b can't absorb genuine closing dynamics) + |b|≤B_MAX clamp (canary-logged); λ_b/B_MAX sized to the ADR-0053 bias-drift RATE. (4) DUAL ACTION: cue_corr = cue_range − b (subtract the bias — reclaims cue precision the hand-set can't) AND a confidence rolloff c_bias that decays cue influence to 0 once |b| exceeds the 0.5 m budget (the safety floor). Cue-emitted VELOCITY is UNCHANGED (a constant offset is derivative-invariant — ADR-0015's #1 lever untouched). (5) BEARING channel byte-untouched (unit test: lam.x_hat bit-unchanged across any WORST-bias cue stream). (6) HONESTY LATCH: b + rng_cam STRUCTURALLY sliced out at handoff (static AST test + per-tick audit cue_reads_post_handoff=0); terminal camera-only, unchanged.
- **WORST-tier defense (HIGH structural confidence; MODERATE on net benefit).** Breaks the bias-lock two independent structural ways: (1) no camera gate exists on the alpha-beta substrate → "reject the true camera" is unreachable; (2) the cue is de-biased/cut BEFORE it can pull the state. A constant spoof IS a bias → absorbed by b; a fast-varying spoof → b can't follow, residual looks like an outlier → the Huber clip rejects it → state stays clean. Either way the offset never enters the state the (nonexistent) gate would protect.
- **Sequencing + pre-registered gate (builder's rule: prototype on mock, adopt only on real data).** STAGE A (mock): offline replay screens FIRST (the ADR-0041 execute-the-estimator-before-flight discipline that caught 2 pre-build failure modes) — new scripts/agreement_monitor_replay.py, sweep --datum-bias-m {0, 0.5, 2.5-WORST, 6.3-the-ADR-0053-bias} × σ_R tiers × Markov camera-dropout; 4 screens: (S1) NO false suppression at datum 0 (c_bias~1 ≥95% ticks), (S2) bias rejection at WORST, (S3) the ADR-0053 range-dependent −6.3 m profile tracked, (S4) lam.x_hat bit-unchanged + b/rng_cam discarded at latch. Only then rank in guidance_lab.py (Lab ranks, Gazebo decides), then a mock Gazebo ladder (mc-batch, markerless v2, monitor vs plain FusedTrack, paired seed-42, n≥8). STAGE B (adopt): only on REAL T17-v2 stereo-cue flights.
- **⚑ HONEST NULL POSSIBILITY (pre-registered).** If T17-v2's retrain brings the real cue's residual bias WITHIN the 0.5 m budget the hand-set FusedTrack ALREADY tolerates, this refinement EARNS NOTHING and the honest outcome is a NULL (default stays plain --fuse-midcourse). That is an acceptable, pre-registered result — the design's value is conditional on the real cue carrying a persistent >0.5 m bias, which T17-v2's outcome directly determines. Do NOT adopt on a wash.
- **Status: DESIGN ratified (judge-panel), NOT built.** Execution pending; first steps = the offline replay screens. Open questions logged (is the real bias slow-along-LOS or discrete-jumping; bias-vs-noise SNR at long range where σ_cam=0.10·R is weak; tuning transfer / the ADR-0018 over-tune-then-fail trap; does the lock-streak gate open too late under markerless dropout).
- **Date:** 2026-07-09 (early). Judge-panel workflow transcript in session logs; grounded in ADR-0041/0044/0053 + the FusedTrack/correct_cue code.

## ADR-0055 — T17-v2 multi-range detector: the ADR-0053 out-of-domain bias is FIXED (held-out-validated), gate PASS — which TRIPS T20's pre-registered NULL for the nominal cue, but leaves the anti-jam datum/spoof lever (ADR-0044) intact
- **What was built.** ground_v2 single-class detector trained on a 7-standoff multi-range dataset (50-160 m, 302 labels), **held out {70, 130} m entirely from training** (a real range-generalization split, unlike v1's ~0.3° interpolation). Orchestrated by scripts/run_t17v2.sh (self-contained: capture → build_ground_dataset_v2 → train → export → check_t17v2.sh) so training blocked in-script, avoiding the agent-loop-wait trap. Ran overnight ~40 min CPU. Weights scripts/seeker/weights/ground_v2.{pt,onnx}.
- **Result — the ADR-0053 bias is FIXED, and it GENERALIZES (held-out proof).** Real-detector centroid bias vs range, v1→v2: 50 m −6.31→**−0.063**; **70 m (HELD OUT) −6.17→−0.010**; 90 m −0.38→+0.40; 110 m +0.39→+0.63; **130 m (HELD OUT) +0.26→−0.14**; 150 m −0.18→+0.23; 160 m +0.61→+0.10. Box-size head now MONOTONIC (158→114→88→72→62→53→50 px over 50→160 m; v1 was non-monotonic — the 50 m box was SMALLER than 90 m). check_t17v2.sh PASS (pre-registered: |bias@50 m| < 2 m AND box-size monotonic). The held-out ranges (never trained) coming in at −0.010/−0.14 m is the real generalization evidence — v1's failure was structural (single operating point), v2 fixes it.
- **Note.** Real-detector σ_R vs R (v2): all-standoffs fit exponent ~1.63 (r²=0.87) — the REAL detector's centroid noise grows a bit slower than the pure-geometry R² (ADR-0053's perfect-centroid 2.003); held-out-only fit is n=2 (not meaningful). σ_R stays sub-metre (0.1-0.9 m) across the envelope. The detector noise scaling is a detector property, distinct from the validated geometry scaling.
- **★ The T20 implication (pre-registered null, honestly triggered).** ADR-0054 pre-registered: *if T17-v2 brings the real cue's residual bias within the 0.5 m budget the hand-set FusedTrack already tolerates, the fusion refinement earns NOTHING (null).* v2's residual bias is ≤0.14 m on held-out ranges (worst any-range 0.63 m @110 m, marginal) → **for the NOMINAL detector-driven cue, T20's de-bias is a NULL — do not adopt --fuse-agreement-monitor as a default on nominal data.** BUT this is the DETECTOR bias only; T20's real ADR-0044 target was the DATUM/SPOOF bias (the WORST 2.5 m GPS-offset / jam scenario, a SEPARATE source T17-v2 did not and could not touch). So T20 is NOT dead — its jam-resistance value (defeating the injected-datum-bias bias-lock) remains the open question, and any T20 mock prototype should focus on the INJECTED WORST-datum/spoof scenario, NOT the nominal cue. Net: the fusion arc's honest headline is now "the retrained detector removed the nominal cue bias; the fusion refinement is reserved for the jam/spoof case it was actually designed for."
- **Decision.** (1) ground_v2 is the RECOMMENDED ground detector (fixes ADR-0049/0053); v1 kept for reproducibility. (2) T20's nominal-cue de-bias = pre-registered NULL (logged, not adopted). (3) T20's anti-jam scenario stays open — if pursued, prototype against injected --datum-bias-m WORST/spoof, not nominal. (4) The multi-range dataset + ground_v2 now provide the truly-held-out data any future real-cue fusion adoption needs.
- **Verifier-CONFIRMED.** Independent re-run of check_t17v2.sh (exit 0, byte-identical deterministic comparison JSON), held-out split verified from split_meta.csv (zero 70/130 m frames in train — structural directory split, genuine range poses not interpolation), detector reads pixels only (gt scoring-only, real IoU matching), v1-vs-v2 apples-to-apples, box-size monotonicity confirmed from raw JSON, real n=13 pairs/standoff (better than ADR-0053's n=5). Fixed a stale "n=5" caveat string (now dynamic). One disclosed nit stands: the 110 m TRAIN-range residual 0.63 m is marginally over the 0.5 m budget (held-out ranges are well under).
- **★ T20 now looks like a FULL null (not just nominal), so it is DEFERRED unbuilt.** The verifier surfaced the decisive point: ADR-0044's hand-set polar FusedTrack ALREADY survived the WORST 2.5 m injected datum bias 8/8 clean (max miss 2.88 m). So T20's anti-jam de-bias (ADR-0054) would have to beat a champion that already clears the WORST bar — and with T17-v2 removing the nominal bias too, the agreement-monitor has no regime left where it demonstrably earns its complexity. **Decision: do NOT build T20; the hand-set FusedTrack (--fuse-midcourse) remains the recommended fusion. ADR-0054's design stands as a ratified-but-unbuilt option, to revisit ONLY if a future scenario the hand-set's fixed 2.5 m budget does NOT cover is prioritized (a persistent bias >2.5 m, or an active time-varying spoof).** This is the pre-registered-null discipline applied honestly: a sound design that the data says isn't needed.
- **Date:** 2026-07-09 (morning; overnight run, interpreted after the 8.5 h gap; verifier-confirmed).

## ADR-0056 — T21 higher-speed + maneuvering (12 m/s weave/jink): markerless+fusion degrades hard, but a clean-AprilTag CONTROL proves it is a PERCEPTION (bearing-quality) limit, NOT kinematic — the maneuver removes the averaging that was hiding the markerless bearing noise
- **Context.** The markerless+fusion arc (ADR-0038..0044) was 9 m/s STRAIGHT-LINE only — a flagged honesty gap. T21 pushes to 12 m/s + weave/jink. Mock cue (real-cue maneuvering caches don't exist yet), markerless seeker (drone_finetuned_v2) + hand-set FusedTrack (--fuse-midcourse), the M5 deployment profile, paired master-seed-42, n=8/arm via mc-batch (weave pilot n=2 first). Plot docs/images/t21_maneuver_miss.png.
- **Result (Pk@2.5 m, ADR-0025; miss median).** MARKERLESS weave: **1/8, median 5.4 m, mean 5.23 m, max 9.7 m**; MARKERLESS jink: **3/8, median 3.7 m, mean 3.98 m**; **APRILTAG weave (the control): 8/8, median 1.64 m (1.6395 — the value quoted portfolio-wide), mean 1.54 m, max 2.33 m.** All arms 8/8 HANDOFF (the cue/fusion delivers the interceptor every time); the failure is purely terminal. Straight-line markerless was ~6/8 / +1 m — so 12 m/s + maneuver roughly 4-5×'s the markerless miss.
- **THE MECHANISM — perception, not kinematic (the control's whole point).** The AprilTag arm, at the SAME 12 m/s weave + SAME guidance/dash config, intercepts 8/8 at 1.64 m — so the guidance law and airframe DEMONSTRABLY catch a 12 m/s maneuvering target; the markerless failure is NOT kinematic. And it is NOT a raw detection-RATE collapse either: BOTH seekers show low terminal coverage (~0.04-0.11) and both break off "lost detection >1.0 s inside terminal range" — the maneuver makes terminal detections SPARSE for both. The difference is per-detection QUALITY: the AprilTag's subpixel-corner bearing converges to a clean intercept even from sparse looks, while the markerless box-center bearing NOISE cannot. So the maneuver's real effect is to REMOVE THE TEMPORAL AVERAGING that, at straight-line, was smoothing the markerless bearing noise down to a just-tolerable +1 m — exactly the "bearing-quality-limited" mechanism ADR-0042/0043 named, now dominant because the maneuver starves the averaging.
- **Corroborating the mechanism.** (1) Direction asymmetry: markerless r2l (median ~5-7.5 m) is much worse than l2r (~2.8-3 m) on BOTH weave and jink; the AprilTag control shows NO such asymmetry (r2l actually tighter) → the asymmetry is a markerless PERCEPTION artifact (a weave-phase × view-geometry interaction with the box-center estimator), not a guidance/geometry effect. (2) Abort lens (scripts/abort_lens.py, PK 2.5 m): every markerless miss is "not aborted", min_gt_range = miss (4-9.7 m) → REAL misses, not post-CPA abort bookkeeping (clean_corrected = raw). (3) Both maneuver types fail the same way → not weave-specific.
- **Decision / implications.** (1) T21's honest headline: **the guidance holds at 12 m/s + maneuver; the markerless terminal seeker's BEARING QUALITY is the binding limit — a perception problem, addressable with a better/faster terminal estimator (subpixel refinement, a tracker to interpolate between sparse detections, or the real Pi/Hailo seeker), NOT a guidance or airframe change.** (2) This is the mechanism behind the arc's straight-line-only results, now measured with a control. (3) The AprilTag arm remains the demonstrable maneuvering-capable configuration (8/8 at 12 m/s weave). (4) T25 video framing: feature the regime that genuinely holds — AprilTag maneuvering and/or markerless straight-line at speed — and let the ground-cue→handoff→camera-only anti-jam story carry; do NOT stage a markerless-maneuvering terminal the seeker cannot hold. (5) Honesty boundary inherited from ADR-0044 (identical markerless+fusion cue path; T21 changes kinematics, not the guidance/cue code, so the no-cheat audit carries — 0 post-latch cue updates by construction).
- **Follow-ups (non-blocking):** the markerless bearing-quality fix (a terminal tracker/subpixel step) is the natural next markerless improvement if pursued; re-run on the REAL stereo cue needs maneuvering trajectory caches (not built); a per-tick audit re-run is a cheap belt if the markerless path is ever modified.
- **Date:** 2026-07-09 (late morning). Arm CSVs logs/mc_t21_*.csv (gitignored); plot committed.
- **Addendum (2026-07-10, forward-audit FWD-A4 — give the portfolio's "jink 1/8" a decisions.md home).** The "jink 1/8 → 14/15" baseline quoted in README/PROGRESS/bullets is THIS ADR's plain-jink arm re-scored POST-HANDOFF camera-terminal: 1/8 at Pk@2.5 (the pooled figure above is 3/8 — different attribution level, same flights; previously recorded only in NEXT.md). Related denominators, each honest with its own scope: the paired n=16 A/B reads 3/15 → 14/15 (ADR-0058), and the post-FIX-A adopted-config jink re-run reads pooled 16/16 (ADR-0062 addendum, different code version — never pool across them). — Markerless maneuver fix ATTEMPT (--terminal-reject-gate) NOT VALIDATED + a self-correction: the terminal false-lock is real but SPORADIC, and the run-to-run noise floor at 12 m/s+maneuver is ~5 m (not ~1 m) — my n=2 pilot "win" was misread NOISE, the gate does not earn adoption at n=8
- **What I tried.** ADR-0056 diagnosed markerless maneuvering misses as gross terminal FALSE LOCKS (meas range ~1.6 m while the true target is ~20 m away; 18-22 m range error across failed weave flights — REAL, re-confirmed). Fix attempt (commit a5b3b56): `--terminal-reject-gate M` — in ENGAGE, reject a fresh detection whose range disagrees with the OWN dead-reckoned range-track (range_filter.x_hat, honesty-clean) by >M m, and coast instead of chasing the phantom.
- **★ SELF-CORRECTION (the important part).** I reported an n=2 pilot as a decisive win ("r2l 6.84->1.11 m, the gate rejected 2 false locks"). That was WRONG, caught by re-reading the pilot log: the 6.84->1.11 m flight was r2l with **terminal_rejects=0** (the gate did NOTHING — the improvement was run-to-run noise); the 2-reject flight was l2r (2.94->1.98 m). Same seed 256788, gate a no-op, produced 6.84 m one run and 1.11 m another → a **~5.7 m run-to-run swing on an IDENTICAL code path**. I violated the project's own "statistics before verdicts" rule (a small-n delta below the noise floor is noise) and mismatched reject-counts to flights. The commit a5b3b56 message is therefore wrong and is superseded by this ADR.
- **The real finding — a HUGE, sporadic noise floor.** The markerless terminal false-lock is a run-to-run COIN FLIP (the NN false-locks on some renders, not others; Gazebo render/RTF nondeterminism makes the exact terminal frames vary run-to-run). So the maneuvering intercept miss is ~5 m variance run-to-run at a FIXED seed, not the ~1 m straight-line floor. This dominates any n=8 comparison and is itself the key result: **the markerless maneuvering failure is a sporadic, violent false-lock, not a systematic degradation.** (ADR-0056's qualitative point — markerless mostly fails, the AprilTag holds 8/8 consistently — still stands; the exact per-flight miss numbers are noisier than presented.)
- **n=8 A/B verdict (gate=5, paired seeds, master-seed 42).** Weave Pk@2.5 1/8->5/8 BUT the improvement is mostly NOISE not the gate — the rejects=0 flights (gate did nothing) improved as much or more (median delta -2.06 m) than the rejects>0 flights (median +0.16 m). Jink REGRESSED (Pk 3/8->2/8, mean/median both worse). Only 4/16 flights ever triggered the gate; of those: 1 clear win (-7.79 m), 1 flat, **2 HARMS (>1 m worse — the gate rejected a GOOD detection)**. 50% harm rate on gate-triggering flights. NOT adopted.
- **Why the gate is weak (mechanism).** It gates against range_filter.x_hat, but the OWN track's range at terminal is itself dead-reckoning a maneuvering target through the latched handoff — not accurate enough to reliably separate a false lock (1.6 m) from a fast-closing real detection. So it both MISSES most false locks (only 4/16 fired) and sometimes rejects real ones (2 harms). The reference is the problem.
- **Decision.** (1) `--terminal-reject-gate` STAYS as a documented, default-OFF, NOT-VALIDATED option (harmless, pins green); do not adopt. (2) The maneuvering fix is REOPENED with two corrected requirements: (a) measure against the ~5 m sporadic-false-lock noise floor — need larger n (n>=16-24) or a variance-reduction, not n=2/8 paired deltas; (b) catch the false lock at the DETECTION level (temporal/size/confidence consistency, or detect-then-track), NOT against the unreliable own-track range. (3) Builder directive stands (video deferred until markerless holds a maneuver, [[markerless-maneuver-fix-directive]]) — this attempt failed honestly; the diagnosis (sporadic detection-level false lock) is the real target.
- **Date:** 2026-07-09 (afternoon). Corrects a5b3b56 + my pilot misread. A/B arms logs/mc_t21_gate_*.csv (gitignored).
- **CHARACTERIZATION addendum (n=16 weave, instrumented per-detection confidence `meas_conf` = decision_margin into the flight CSV; Opus subagent).** The finding reframes the fix: over ~217 ENGAGE detections across 16 weave flights, **195 are FALSE (range err >8 m) vs only 5 GOOD (<=3 m)** — at 12 m/s+maneuver the markerless terminal seeker produces ESSENTIALLY NO usable detections; clean flights HIT by COASTING on the camera track, dirty flights CHASE the phantoms (misses have 26-47 false detections; hits have 0-5). Two decisive sub-findings: (1) **CONFIDENCE IS INVERTED and useless as a gate** — FALSE locks median conf 0.34 vs GOOD 0.17 (the big phantom box is a HIGH-confidence false positive; the real ~8 px far target is LOW-confidence). A confidence gate would keep the phantoms and drop the good — confidence is DEAD as a fix. (2) The false locks DRIVE the misses (strong count-vs-miss correlation). **So the only viable lever is a CONSISTENCY gate — reject detections grossly inconsistent with a PROTECTED, dead-reckoned, camera-only range coast** (attempt-2). This fixes attempt-1's bug: attempt-1 gated against range_filter.x_hat which the first phantom CORRUPTED; attempt-2 snapshots the camera range at handoff (range_filter/lambda_filter are camera-only, never cue-corrected — honesty verified) and dead-reckons it forward UNCORRUPTED, so the ~18 m-off phantoms are reliably rejected. This IS the camera-only "temporal fusion to reject false positives" the builder asked for. Fix + n=16 A/B next.
- **ROOT-CAUSE addendum (attempts 1&2 both failed structurally; re-diagnosis).** Attempts 1 (terminal range-gate) and 2 (protected-coast) BOTH failed because they fought the phantom AFTER the damage was done. The n=16 root-cause analysis: the phantom triggers a FALSE HANDOFF — the markerless seeker false-locks during DASH, the phantom reads in-range (~1.8 m), latches camera-only while the TRUE target is 15-100 m away. Phantom handoff **12/16 (miss median 4.53 m)** vs real-ish handoff **3/16 (miss median 1.75 m)** — the handoff detection IS the outcome. Attempt-2's coast gate barely fired (14/16 flights 0 rejects) because its reference was seeded from the phantom handoff itself.
- **Attempt-3 (cue-validated handoff, --handoff-cue-gate M): a real +Pk but a CRUTCH, NOT the jam-resistant fix.** Gate the handoff-streak increment against the cue's target position (legal PRE-handoff; honesty-clean, no gt). Weave n=16 (M=8): phantom handoffs **12→2**, Pk@2.5 **50%→75%**, miss median 2.59→2.27. BUT the handoff-OUTCOME breakdown reveals it: real handoffs stayed 4→3; the 10 avoided phantoms became **NEVER-HANDED-OFF (11/16)** — those flights ride the CUE to impact (cue-terminal = comms-available, NOT the comms-denied camera-only capability). And 2 of the 3 real handoffs still missed 6-7 m. So the Pk lift is CUE-RELIANT: the gate stops the interceptor being fooled onto empty space, and riding the cue beats the phantom-terminal — but the markerless CAMERA still cannot acquire/track the fast maneuverer. `--handoff-cue-gate` kept default-OFF as a documented comms-available option, NOT adopted as the maneuver fix.
- **PIVOT (builder's insight): DETECT-THEN-TRACK.** The builder correctly identified the real fix: NN IDENTIFIES the target, a cheap VISUAL TRACKER (cv2 CSRT/KCF) FOLLOWS the moving object frame-to-frame — denser than the ~14 Hz NN, temporally consistent, ignores phantoms — seeded from a cue-validated NN detection pre-handoff, then camera-only in the terminal (jam-resistant). Also clarified: the NN isn't too SLOW (phantoms are 18 m-off false positives, not lag — a faster chip gives more phantoms, not better tracking); markerless pro-nav DID get close intercepts STRAIGHT-LINE (ADR-0038..0044), the 12 m/s+maneuver is the specific out-of-regime case. Build + A/B (structural metrics — real-handoff rate + terminal meas-vs-gt, not just miss) in progress.

## ADR-0058 — DETECT-THEN-TRACK markerless terminal (NN acquires ONCE, cv2 CSRT follows, NN re-validates every 8 frames): the maneuvering false-lock fix that WORKS — post-handoff camera-terminal Pk@2.5 recovered weave 3/16→10/15 and jink 3/15→14/15 with phantom handoffs 12→2; plus the two-Fable-review-hardened honesty / RTF / cap fixes
- **The problem (ADR-0056/0057).** At 12 m/s + weave/jink the markerless NN throws gross PHANTOMS — a big, HIGH-confidence box ~18 m off the true target — intermixed with a few LOW-confidence good looks at the real few-pixel target. Confidence is INVERTED (phantom median 0.34 > real 0.17), so a confidence gate is structurally DEAD. The phantom triggers a FALSE HANDOFF (reads in-range ~1.8 m while the truth is 15-100 m away), the camera latches onto empty space, and the interceptor misses 4-9 m — while a clean AprilTag CONTROL holds 8/8 at 1.64 m, proving the limit is BEARING/PERCEPTION, not kinematic. Attempts 1-3 (terminal range-gate, protected-coast, cue-validated handoff) each failed or were comms-available crutches (ADR-0057).
- **The fix — builder's insight, DETECT-THEN-TRACK.** The NN IDENTIFIES the target ONCE (validated so the seed lands on the REAL target, not a phantom), then a cheap VISUAL TRACKER (cv2 CSRT — scale-adaptive, so range-from-box-width stays live) FOLLOWS that same blob frame-to-frame: temporally consistent, denser than the ~14 Hz NN, and it IGNORES phantom NN boxes elsewhere in the frame. Every 8 frames the NN re-validates (IoU-overlap → re-seed to correct drift; a DISAGREEING NN box re-seeds pre-handoff ONLY if the cue confirms it, and post-handoff is ignored — the anti-phantom rule). This IS the camera-only "temporal fusion to reject false positives" the builder asked for. Code: `scripts/seeker/detect_track.py` (DetectThenTracker + SeekerSeedContext), wired via `m4_intercept.py --track` (requires `--handoff`; default OFF, byte-identical when off; `markerless_loop.py` constructs the wrapper only under --track).
- **Honesty boundary (re-earned, ADR-0010).** The tracker reads camera pixels + fixed intrinsics ONLY; the SEED may use the external cue ONLY pre-handoff (its legitimate job — guide the dash until the camera truly acquires). SeekerSeedContext enforces this with TWO belts: `update()` forces `cue_pos=None` once handoff_done (the null-under-latch), AND every consumer reads via `legal_cue_pos()`, which RAISES if a cue is ever observable post-latch — a raise kills the detection thread, measurements stop, and guidance coasts/aborts (fail-safe, never fly on illegal data). Both belts are AST-pinned + runtime-exercised in `tests/test_honesty_static.py`; the module never touches `gt_*`.
- **What the two Fable reviews found.** (1) POOLED-Pk ATTRIBUTION INFLATION — `miss_m` is a WHOLE-FLIGHT running min, so pooled Pk credits CPAs banked during the legal cue-guided DASH; flattering and NOT the comms-denied camera-only capability. Fix: report Pk THREE ways — (i) pooled whole-flight, (ii) POST-HANDOFF min gt (the camera-terminal metric), (iii) REAL-handoff-conditioned (terminal-attributable) — via `scripts/analyze_track_ab.py`, which also classifies each handoff never/REAL/RANGE_BIASED_REAL/PHANTOM. (2) INVERTED confidence → NO conf gate anywhere in the tracker. (3) WALL-TIME coast aging — the re-acquire radius was aged with `det.t_mono` (= time.monotonic), an ADR-0009 violation: under RTF sag it grew 2-3× too fast per sim-second. (4) REACQ cap 20 m > the ~18 m phantom offset the gate exists to reject.
- **Review-mandated fixes applied (this ADR's delta, BEFORE the headline arm).** (a) RTF-INVARIANT COAST CLOCK: the re-acquire radius now ages on FRAMES-since-loss × a nominal sim-period (default 1/14 s ≈ the detector's measured ~14 Hz cadence), never wall time — the SAME frame-count basis the every-8-frames re-validation already used and the review praised as RTF-invariant. (b) REACQ CAP default 20 → **12 m** (< the ~18 m phantom offset), so the phantom stays rejected at EVERY coast time, not just short coasts. Plus review MINORS: `MARKERLESS_TRACK_KIND` validated in the MAIN thread (new `validate_track_kind()`) BEFORE the detector thread starts — unknown value = loud immediate error, KCF refused with the frozen-range reason (no scale adaptation), instead of a silent detector-thread death; a once-only detector-thread liveness WARNING in the control loop (observability; the 0.4 s MEAS_STALE gate already fails safe); and the null-under-latch honesty pin TIGHTENED from "some assignment matches" to "ALL match" + a mutation calibration test (the review mutation-proved an appended second `self.cue_pos =` bypassed the old pin).
- **Three-way attribution results (n=16 paired, master-seed 42; track arms flew PRE-FIX code = conservative FLOOR).** WEAVE, track vs plain: phantom handoffs **12/16 → 2/16**; POST-HANDOFF camera-terminal Pk@2.5 **3/16 → 10/15** (median **13.29 → 2.28 m**); REAL-conditioned 2/4 → 10/13; terminal ENGAGE dets 5 GOOD / 198 FALSE → **71 GOOD / 15 FALSE**; paired median **−3.22 m** (A=track better). JINK, track vs plain: POST-HANDOFF Pk **3/15 → 14/15** (median **3.46 → 1.97 m**); REAL-conditioned 13/13; paired median **−1.61 m** (the jink plain baseline actually GOT real handoffs — 90 GOOD/0 FALSE dets — but still missed at 3.46 m; the tracker's temporal density recovers it, showing the win is not only phantom-rejection). AprilTag CONTROL 8/8 median 1.64 m = the perception ceiling; detect-then-track now lands weave/jink at ~1.97-2.28 m, within ~0.3-0.6 m of it. Pooled Pk is 15/16 both track arms (flattering — includes legal cue-banked hits; always report all three levels). Logs (gitignored): `logs/mc_t21_{track_weave12,track_jink12,plain_jink12_n16,char_weave12,apriltag_weave12}.csv`; analyzer `scripts/analyze_track_ab.py`.
- **Env knobs (all `MARKERLESS_TRACK_*`, read at DetectThenTracker init so the A/B sweeps without a code edit).** KIND (CSRT default; MIL; KCF refused), SEED_GATE_M (8 — the pre-handoff cue-consistency seed radius, matches `--handoff-cue-gate`), REVALIDATE (8 frames), RESEED_IOU (0.2), REACQ_BASE_M (8), REACQ_RATE_M_S (5), **REACQ_CAP_M (12, was 20)**, **FRAME_DT_S (1/14 — the coast-clock nominal sim-period)**.
- **Bounded residuals (micro-review verdict, accepted as documentation — no code change).** (1) The frame-count coast clock is honest but the code comment's "RTF-invariant" OVERCLAIMS: FRAME_DT_S=1/14 embeds the RTF≈1 NN cadence, and under RTF sag the ACQUIRE loop becomes CAMERA-bound (the camera publishes 30 Hz in SIM time), so the coast clock can run up to ~30/14 ≈ 2.1× fast in sim-seconds across the 8 m base → 12 m cap ramp. The CAP carries the anti-phantom guarantee (the ~18 m phantom is rejected at EVERY coast age regardless of clock speed) and batch arms fly at RTF≈1, so this is not batch-invalidating; soften the comment on the next legitimate edit of detect_track.py. (2) Reject-side trade at the 12 m cap: a REAL re-acquire after a ~2 s coast can brush the cap and be rejected — deliberate (the MEAS_STALE→hold→abort ladder backs the reject side: coasting on nothing is safe, re-seeding on a phantom is not); `n_reacq_rejected` in the tracker stats line is the watch-metric for whether it ever bites.
- **Caveat + the pre-registered headline arm.** The track arms above flew PRE-FIX code (wall-time aging + cap-20), so their numbers are a conservative FLOOR — the two fixes only TIGHTEN phantom rejection, they cannot lower it. The HEADLINE ARM re-flies WEAVE n=16 on POST-FIX code with the deployment config `--track` + `--handoff-cue-gate 8` (the cue gates the handoff MOMENT only, legal pre-handoff; the two anti-phantom levers combined) as the pre-registered deployment-config validation → `logs/mc_t21_trackgate_weave12_r2.csv`. **Run provenance (honest): the first headline attempt (r1, `logs/mc_t21_trackgate_weave12.csv`) is INVALID — a launch-args error, not a feature failure: the relaunch was rebuilt from the skill template without the markerless env set (`MC_SEEKER/MC_WORLD/MC_TARGET_MODEL/MC_VENV_PYTHON`), so mc_batch passed `--seeker apriltag` + `--track` and m4's own ADR-0058 validation correctly refused it — 16 argparse-rejected boots, breakoff_reason='python_exit_2', zero flights, no data. Superseded by r2 (corrected env, argv argparse-smoked pre-launch).** Commit is SPECIFIC-paths-only AFTER the Fable micro-review of this fix delta (never `git add -A`; `yolo11n.pt`, `.claude/settings.local.json`, and the v3-dataset files excluded as a separate commit).
- **★ HEADLINE ARM RESULTS (r2, post-fix code, `--track` + `--handoff-cue-gate 8`, weave 12 m/s n=16 paired master-seed 42 — the pre-registered deployment-config validation): CLEAN SWEEP.** Pooled Pk@2.5 **16/16** (med 2.11, max 2.48 m); POST-HANDOFF camera-terminal Pk **14/14** (med 2.03, max 2.48 m); REAL-conditioned **14/14**. Handoff outcomes REAL=13 / RANGE_BIASED_REAL=1 / never=2 — **ZERO phantom handoffs**, and the terminal detection pool is **155 dets, 0 FALSE (>8 m)** — the first arm in the whole T21 maneuvering campaign with zero gross false terminal detections. `n_reacq_rejected=0` on all 16 flights (the 12 m cap never bit a real re-acquire — the pre-registered watch-metric is clean); `phantoms_ignored=13` total (the re-validation anti-phantom rule actively working). The 2 never-handoff flights, characterized from their logs: run 10 = the cue-gate REFUSING a phantom lock exactly as designed (28 in-range handoff refusals with camera-implied position >8 m off the cue — the reads-close/is-far phantom signature; the flight rode the LEGAL pre-handoff cue dash to a 2.14 m pooled CPA, then aborted; honestly counted NOT camera-terminal); run 12 = the camera simply never acquired in-range (0 gate rejects, best streak 0, coverage 5% — a sparse-detection flight, not a gate refusal; legal-dash CPA 2.16 m). The 2.57 m late latch (run 4) = a cue-consistent LATE acquisition (first dash detection 12.46 m, 0 gate rejects), latched on the early-handoff streak, camera-only terminal delivered 2.27 m — a hit, not an artifact. Paired vs track_weave: pooled miss is a WASH (median +0.01 m — both configs sit at the ~2 m perception ceiling); the gate's earn is TAIL ELIMINATION (post-handoff max 5.97 → 2.48 m, phantom handoffs 2 → 0, FALSE dets 15 → 0). Paired vs plain weave n=16: pooled median −0.49 / mean −2.20 m, camera-terminal Pk 3/16 → 14/14. **Verdict: detect-then-track + `--handoff-cue-gate 8` ADOPTED as the deployment config for the markerless maneuvering regime; the builder's maneuver-fix directive is SATISFIED — the markerless seeker honestly holds a maneuvering camera-only terminal (14/14 post-latch at 12 m/s weave).** Arm CSV `logs/mc_t21_trackgate_weave12_r2.csv`; GPU healthy across the batch (dxgk 4081→4095, benign escape noise only, no orphans). **Forward note (2026-07-10):** this arm's n=16 framing only supports a 79.4% CP-LB — later HARDENED to n=72 (Pk ≥95% CI at R=2.8 m) by ADR-0064; the mechanism conclusion here (detect-then-track fixes the maneuvering false-lock) is unaffected.
- **Date:** 2026-07-09 (evening/night). Feature `scripts/seeker/detect_track.py` + `m4_intercept.py --track`; review verdict `docs/review_track_fix_delta_20260709.md` (file retired 2026-07-10, ADR-0066 — recover via `git show e6f06d3:docs/review_track_fix_delta_20260709.md`); full suite 71 passed / 2 skipped (66 baseline + 5 honesty tripwires).

## ADR-0059 — FINDING + FIX (IMPLEMENTED, pending MC validation): the adopted deployment config `--track --handoff-cue-gate 8` FAILS CLOSED under a mid-dash cue jam — the ADR-0057/0058 anti-phantom gates silently re-coupled terminal handoff to a LIVE cue with no staleness handling, a regression against the WORST-tier link-cutoff the project already built coast-search to survive. Found by the multi-agent design review, mechanism CONFIRMED by an independent code-trace; fix IMPLEMENTED 2026-07-09 (sim-time staleness age-out + camera-only fallback, default-ON but inert under a live cue), unit + honesty tested (full suite 86 passed / 2 skipped), and the paired comms-denied MC jam-arm harness built (`scripts/mc_jam_arm.sh`, NOT yet flown — idle-load rule, sim gated on the v3 fine-tune).
- **What it is.** The design review (`docs/design_review_sim_to_real_2026-07-10.md`, gap G3a) flagged that the flagship comms-denied claim may fail closed. An independent read-only code-trace (Opus) confirmed the mechanism against the actual code and corrected the review's threat-model framing. VERDICT: **PARTIALLY-CONFIRMED** — real for the deployment/headline config, does NOT apply to the default config, and mid-dash jam is IN the stated threat model (not out-of-scope).
- **The mechanism (confirmed, line-traced).** `last_cue_pos` (m4_intercept.py: init 1767, assigned ONLY at 1997 under `if cue_is_new AND not fresh`) has NO expiry/age-out anywhere. When the cue jams, `CueReader.read()` keeps returning the last tuple forever, so `cue_is_new` goes False and `last_cue_pos` FREEZES at the last pre-jam value. Two independent walls then reject the REAL target: (1) the **handoff cue-gate** (m4:2356-2371, active only when `--handoff-cue-gate` is set): `cue_gap = |cam_implied_tgt − last_cue_pos|` grows ~12 m/s as the true target moves away from the frozen point; once >8 m (~0.67 s after the jam) every in-range detection is rejected → `handoff_streak` pinned at 0 → never hands off. (2) the **seed gate** (detect_track.py `_seed_ok` pre-handoff branch 283-288, active under `--track` ALONE, independent of the cue-gate): a frozen-cue `cue_gap > seed_cue_gate_m` (8 m) blocks the tracker from even SEEDING on the real target → no track → no measurement → streak never advances. The deployment arm has BOTH → double fail-closed. NUANCE: the hole is the **jammed-AFTER-first-fix** case; if the cue never delivered a datagram, `last_cue_pos` stays None and `_seed_ok` falls through to the camera-track-continuity branch (harmless).
- **Default vs deployment (the review's split is correct).** `--handoff-cue-gate` default = None (m4:1475). **Default config under jam = HANDS OFF FINE** (pure camera-only N-consecutive-in-range streak, zero cue dependency) — but is vulnerable to the DASH phantom false-handoff ADR-0057/0058 exist to fix. **Deployment config (ADR-0058 r2, `--track --handoff-cue-gate 8`) under jam = NEVER hands off.** So there is a genuine TENSION: the config that beats phantoms (cue-gated) is the one that fails under jam; the config that survives jam (camera-only) is the one that eats phantoms. Resolving that tension — camera-only anti-phantom under a stale cue — is the real work, not a one-liner.
- **Threat-model scoping (corrects the review).** Mid-dash jam is NOT out-of-scope: ADR-0015 enumerates "Link cutoff before lock — jammer-controlled cutoff time/range" as a WORST-credible tier, with a purpose-built `--link-cutoff`/dead-reckon-coast/bounded-search branch (`--coast-search`, m4:2220-2235) and the `--jam-envelope` study (ADR-0026) sizing a link-cut at ~11.5 m before lock. The "simulate worse than ideal" mandate requires decisions to survive WORST. The ADR-0057/0058 anti-phantom gates (added later) re-coupled the terminal handoff to a live cue with no staleness handling — a **regression against the exact tier coast-search targets**. It is a REAL, IN-SCOPE hole for the deployment config, but **LATENT/UNTESTED**: every ADR-0058 arm ran the cue mock full-duration (no jam), so the gate code has never been run against a mid-dash jam. Honest portfolio consequence: "works comms-denied" must be HELD (currently fails closed under pre-acquisition jam in the adopted config) until the fix + jam MC arm land.
- **The fix (design; pieces already in-tree — NOT yet applied).** Age-out `last_cue_pos` on cue staleness and fall back to the camera-only paths that already exist: the staleness clock is present (`last_cue_recv_sim_t` m4:1976 + `COAST_STALE_S`=1.0 m4:503, already computed as `cue_age_dash` in the coast-search branch 2226); the camera-only fallbacks are present (handoff gate's default `cue_consistent=True` camera-only streak; `_seed_ok`'s post-handoff `cam_track`-continuity branch with a coast-aging radius, detect_track.py 289-312). THE CHANGE: at the gate read (m4:2356) and the seed feed (m4:2723), when `cue_age > COAST_STALE_S` (or a dedicated jam horizon) treat the cue as ABSENT — pass `cue_pos=None` into `seed_ctx.update` (routes `_seed_ok` into the cam-track-continuity branch) and skip the handoff cue gate (camera-only streak). Mechanically small, localized to two read sites. HONEST CAVEAT: falling back to camera-only under a stale cue REOPENS the phantom-false-handoff risk the gate closes, so the sound version leans on the tracker-continuity + ADR-0056 own-range-track reject as the anti-phantom lever under jam — it deserves its own ADR + a jam MC arm, NOT a blind one-liner.
- **Implementation (2026-07-09, this session — builds and unit-tests only; NO sim flown, v3 fine-tune saturating the CPU).** Pure sim-time predicate `cue_is_stale(last_cue_recv_sim_t, sim_now, horizon)` (m4, module-level, unit-tested; ADR-0009 sim-time-only, AST-pinned no wall clock) is evaluated ONCE per tick as `cue_is_stale_now = (not handoff_done) and cue_is_stale(last_cue_recv_sim_t, sim_now, --cue-stale-horizon)` (default `COAST_STALE_S`=1.0). The `not handoff_done` guard (Fable-review defect fix) forces it False post-handoff — staleness is only meaningful PRE-handoff (the cue channel is closed one-way at the latch, so `last_cue_recv_sim_t` freezes on every normal flight and the raw predicate would false-fire ~1 s into ENGAGE); the guard keeps the marker/notice a clean pre-handoff-jam signal and post-handoff guidance + CSV byte-identical. Two localized read-site changes: (1) the **handoff cue-gate** gains `and not cue_is_stale_now` → when stale the whole cue-consistency block drops, `cue_consistent` stays True, handoff falls back to the camera-only N-consecutive-in-range streak (the default-config path); (2) the **seeker seed feed** passes `seed_cue_pos = None if cue_is_stale_now else last_cue_pos` into `seed_ctx.update` → `_seed_ok` routes into its camera-track-continuity branch. Plus a one-time "cue STALE → camera-only" notice + a CSV `cue_stale=1` marker on the jam ticks (guarded `not args.coast_search`, so coast-search owns that column untouched). **INERTNESS (the critical safety property):** the fix path is entered ONLY when `cue_is_stale_now`, structurally False whenever the cue is fresh AND (post-guard) whenever handoff is done, so both read-sites take their exact pre-ADR-0059 branch → **guidance-IDENTICAL when the cue is fresh**, and the post-handoff guard keeps the marker/notice/CSV rows unchanged too (all 71 pinned tests stay byte-identical, confirmed). Proven at the unit level (cue_is_stale False for fresh/no-cue/no-clock) and re-provable in-sim by the `control_strict` arm (fix disabled via a large horizon → **PAIRED-per-seed miss equivalence to r2, NOT an exact 16/16 count** — r2's max miss 2.48 m sits under the 2.5 m line and ~1 m single-flight noise makes 15/16-by-noise plausible). HONEST NUANCE: the realistic cue's `--dropout-markov` P(burst>1 s)≈0.5, so under a live-but-dropping cue the fallback DOES engage on long NORMAL bursts (safe camera-only dead-reckon until recovery — exactly what `control_active` checks); strict byte-identity holds for the fresh-cue path always and for a no-long-dropout cue. **ANTI-PHANTOM UNDER JAM:** the fallback does NOT re-admit phantoms — the seed's camera-track-continuity gate (`_seed_ok`, 8 m radius around the dead-reckoned camera track, hard-capped by REACQ_CAP < the ~18 m phantom offset) rejects the ~18 m phantom, and because a rejected seed = no track = no measurement, the seed gate also protects the handoff even where the cue-gate is bypassed (the two levers are complementary, not redundant). **HONESTY (re-earned):** once stale the frozen `last_cue_pos` is structurally dropped at BOTH sites — no cue-derived value reaches the seed decision (the fallback is camera-only + dead-reckoned own-state, the same legality as `--coast-search`/`--warm-handoff`); no `gt_*`; the ADR-0058 null-under-latch in `SeekerSeedContext.update` is untouched. New tripwire `tests/test_cue_staleness.py` (15 tests, AST-pinned wiring): inertness, anti-fail-closed, anti-phantom, a regression witness (a frozen cue rejects the real target), the honesty tripwire (a frozen cue sitting on a phantom would have admitted it — under the fix it has ZERO influence and the phantom is rejected on the camera track alone), and the post-handoff-guard pin (`cue_is_stale_now` must be `(not handoff_done) and …`). Files: `scripts/m4_intercept.py` (+`cue_is_stale`, `--cue-stale-horizon`, guarded predicate, two read-sites, marker/notice), `scripts/seeker/detect_track.py` (COMMENT-ONLY: the no-cue branch now documents the ADR-0059 stale-cue pre-handoff caller; softened the ADR-0058-flagged "RTF-invariant" overclaim), `tests/test_cue_staleness.py` (new). Full suite **86 passed / 2 skipped** (71 baseline + 15 new).
- **MC jam-arm harness (`scripts/mc_jam_arm.sh`, BUILT not flown).** Four PAIRED arms (identical geometry + master-seed 42 = the same 16 flights; reproduced exactly from the r2 headline stdout log): `control_strict` (no cutoff, fix inert via `--cue-stale-horizon 1e9` → no-regression proof as **paired-per-seed miss equivalence to r2, not an exact count**), `control_active` (no cutoff, fix live → handoff/Pk equivalence to r2, proves the always-on fix is safe under the realistic `--dropout-markov` cue), `jam_fixoff` (mid-dash `--link-cutoff-range-m 15` + fix off → the FAIL-CLOSED witness), `jam_fixon` (cutoff + fix live → the FIX). PRIMARY metric = **HANDOFF-COUNT + post-handoff Pk CONDITIONED ON A REAL HANDOFF** via `scripts/analyze_track_ab.py` (the `never` outcome = the fail-closed count), NOT pooled `count(miss≤2.5)` — a range-cutoff no-handoff dead-reckon flyby can inflate the pooled count, so it over-credits. Cutoff is range-based at 15 m (pre-acquisition: HANDOFF_RANGE_M=10, ADR-0026 envelope ~11.5), the jam bite-point randomized across flights by per-seed weave/geometry; `CUTOFF_RANGE_M=…` sweeps the exposed window. Runs at IDLE, ONE arm at a time, via the mc-batch skill, AFTER the v3 train.
- **Residual risk (honest).** (1) The camera-only fallback leans entirely on the dead-reckoned camera track (`target_tracker.pos_hat`) for its anti-phantom gate; if the pre-jam cue never gave the tracker a good velocity, the dead-reckoned track can drift and the 8 m seed radius could reject a real re-acquire (coasting, safe) or, less likely, admit a wrong lock — the REACQ_CAP bounds the latter and `n_reacq_rejected` is the watch-metric. (2) HORIZON = 1.0 s (= COAST_STALE_S), DECISIVE (Fable ruling, geometry-driven, overrules an earlier lean toward a larger default): a frozen cue is already harmful at ~0.67 s (8 m gate ÷ 12 m/s), so a larger horizon protects nothing beyond that; and cutoff-15 m → handoff-10 m at 12–28 m/s leaves only a ~1–2 s terminal window, so a horizon >1.5–2 s would make the fix a NO-OP for exactly the WORST-tier jam it targets. One concept, one number. Consequence: the realistic `--dropout-markov` cue (P(burst>1 s)≈0.5) also trips the fallback on long NORMAL bursts — SAFE (camera-only dead-reckon until recovery), identical to what `--coast-search` already does at this threshold; so `control_active` may not be bit-for-bit r2 but should be handoff/Pk-equivalent, and it is the data-driven CONFIRMATION (tune ONLY on a real paired regression beyond noise), NOT the default-setter. `control_strict` (`--cue-stale-horizon 1e9`) is the strict byte-identity reference. (3) True per-flight RANDOM cutoff ranges with paired seeds are not built (mc_batch generates its seed family per-invocation); a fixed range with geometry-randomized bite timing + a threshold sweep is the buildable-today equivalent — the design review's own remedy was a future `--cue-kill-range` knob. NEXT: fly the harness at idle, Fable review of the diff, verifier gate.
- **Status / plan (task #31).** Fix IMPLEMENTED + unit/honesty-tested + harness built (this session). REMAINING: (b) fly the paired MC arms at idle after v3 (the corrected metric = intercepts completed under denial); (c) Fable review of this diff (honesty boundary re-earned — camera-only, no post-latch cue); (d) verifier gate. Until (b)-(d) land, "works comms-denied" stays HELD for the deployment config. Design review Action 2 (task #31) is exactly this campaign.
- **Date:** 2026-07-10 finding; 2026-07-09 fix implemented (build + unit/honesty tests + MC harness; sim validation pending, idle-gated behind the v3 fine-tune). This is the design review + adversarial-verify process working end-to-end: the review flagged it (with imprecise threat framing), the independent trace confirmed the mechanism and corrected the framing, and the fix was built against that trace with the inertness/anti-phantom/honesty properties tested up front.
- **★ RESULTS / CLOSE (2026-07-10 — the MC FLEW; supersedes the header's "pending MC validation"): fail-closed DEMONSTRATED with a clean dose-response witness; the fix is VALIDATED AS FAIL-SAFE, NOT as recovery; "works comms-denied" stays HELD.** Scope: speed-12/gate-8 ONLY, n=16 paired flights per arm (master-seed 42, weave), **8 arms** (control_strict, control_active, jam_fixoff/jam_fixon × link-cutoff-range 15/18/22 m), idle load, GPU health flat 4095 across arms, flown on the working tree (fix code NOT yet committed). Link cutoff fired 16/16 in every jam arm (G2 PASS all).
  - **VERDICT (pre-registered `check_jam_mc.py`): NOT VALIDATED — and that is the honest, correct answer.** The 18 m and 22 m verdict runs both exit 1 (the 15 m run also returns NOT VALIDATED, its G3a failing in the OPPOSITE direction — the witness doesn't bite there, see envelope). The pre-registered G3b tests the FULL-RECOVERY hypothesis (fixon REAL-ish ≥11/16); the actual result is fail-safe, not recovery — so the answer to "does the fix RESTORE the comms-denied intercept?" is **NO** (weak-to-nil recovery without `--coast-search`).
  - **Recovery envelope** (PRIMARY metric = REAL-ish handoffs + post-handoff gt, per the harness design above; raw handoff counts + PHANTOM labels alongside). No-jam controls: strict REAL-ish 7/16, active REAL-ish 11/16 — the REAL-ish ceiling is depressed even with NO jam by the noisy range-channel PHANTOM label (strict's own PHANTOM=6/16; see caveats). Per depth:
    - **15 m:** fixoff REAL-ish **12/16** (post-gt Pk 9/14, median 2.19 m) vs fixon **3/16** → **fail-closed DOES NOT MANIFEST** (the link dies only ~5 m before the 10 m handoff; frozen-cue drift stays under the 8 m gate), and the fix carries a **PREMATURE-AGE-OUT COST** here (handoffs 14→10; R8 lost-by-fix=9) because the frozen cue is still usable. The 15 m fixon PHANTOM=7 are range-channel-noise labels on GOOD intercepts — of its 10 handoffs, post-gt Pk 9/10 at median 2.23 m — NOT fooled-by-stale-cue phantoms.
